# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 031 RANDOM TERROR EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# Event-local runtime for the global Random Terror crisis.  The system keeps
# country pressure and response legitimacy separate from state activity, then
# promotes later network, territorial, and readiness values only through
# witnessed incidents, actor control, and accepted route actions.
#
# The automatic entry point is `random_terror_run_global_wave`.  It is called
# once by the Event 031 pacing dispatch and fans out to a bounded set of valid
# country/state targets.  Child report events never call event-system pacing.
# Actor carriers are ordinary predeclared country tags with existing HOI4 unit
# types; the dynamic package setup is transactional and can be cleaned up.
# ============================================================================

# Legacy weight reference values; runtime pools load their matching shared
# script constants into dedicated temporary variables before every draw.
# random_list accepts variable-valued keys (vanilla effects documentation).
@rt_incident_weight_civilian_attack = 16
@rt_incident_weight_transport_disruption = 12
@rt_incident_weight_depot_sabotage = 10
@rt_incident_weight_port_convoy_attack = 8
@rt_incident_weight_official_assassination_attempt = 7
@rt_incident_weight_hostage_crisis = 7
@rt_incident_weight_security_site_attack = 12
@rt_incident_weight_propaganda_surge = 10
@rt_incident_weight_arms_theft = 8
@rt_incident_weight_sponsor_evidence = 6
@rt_incident_weight_smuggling_corridor = 6
@rt_incident_weight_training_area = 5
@rt_incident_weight_copycat = 5
@rt_incident_weight_failed_raid = 5
@rt_incident_weight_intelligence_breakthrough = 4
@rt_incident_weight_defection = 4
@rt_incident_weight_relief_strain = 5
@rt_incident_weight_military_defection = 4
@rt_incident_weight_capital_infiltration = 5
@rt_incident_weight_rival_organization = 4
@rt_incident_weight_community_rejection = 5
@rt_incident_weight_joint_operation = 4
@rt_incident_weight_sponsor_exposure = 4
@rt_incident_weight_cannibal_clash = 2
@rt_scenario_pattern_weight = 25
@rt_raid_clean_weight = 65
@rt_raid_costly_weight = 20

# Core registries and value maintenance
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

random_terror_initialize_global_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_evolution_stage } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_network_reach } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_international_unity } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_territorial_control } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_crisis_state_count } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_crisis_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_territorial_state_count } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_strategic_victories } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_strategic_victories = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_total_incidents } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_total_incidents = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_automatic_wave_id } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_automatic_wave_id = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_last_wave_affected_count } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_last_wave_affected_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_ordinary_cosmetic_sequence } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_ordinary_cosmetic_sequence = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_jihadist_cosmetic_sequence } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_jihadist_cosmetic_sequence = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_transnational_cosmetic_sequence } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_transnational_cosmetic_sequence = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	set_global_flag = random_terror_system_initialized
}

random_terror_initialize_country_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_pressure } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_pressure = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_response_legitimacy } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_response_legitimacy = constant:random_terror_value.medium }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_incident_count } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_incident_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_response_capacity } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = random_terror_lasting_capacity }
			set_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = random_terror_lasting_capacity }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_movement_restrictions_uses } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_movement_restrictions_uses = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		random_terror_update_movement_restrictions_cost = yes
	}
	random_terror_achievement_initialize_country = yes
}

random_terror_initialize_state_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_state_activity } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_state_stage } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.clear }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_state_pressure } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
}

# Country scope. Event 31 owns the caller-side attribution and supplies every
# shared Condemnation input explicitly. The shared system remains responsible
# for scaling, history, public detail, sanctions, and participant refreshes.
random_terror_add_condemnation_source = {
	set_temp_variable = { condemnation_source_type = random_terror_condemnation_source_type }
	set_temp_variable = { condemnation_base_gain = random_terror_condemnation_base_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { condemnation_visibility_type = random_terror_condemnation_visibility_type }
	set_temp_variable = { condemnation_severity_type = random_terror_condemnation_severity_type }
	set_temp_variable = { condemnation_civilian_deaths = random_terror_condemnation_civilian_deaths }
	set_temp_variable = { condemnation_contamination_value = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { condemnation_context_type = random_terror_condemnation_context_type }
	condemnation_add_source = yes
}

random_terror_add_public_government_abuse_condemnation = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_atrocity_or_coverup
	set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_collective_punishment
	set_country_flag = random_terror_government_abuse_condemnation_recorded
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_source_type = constant:condemnation_source.atrocity }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_base_gain = constant:random_terror_tuning.government_condemnation_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_visibility_type = constant:condemnation_visibility.public }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_severity_type = constant:condemnation_severity.major }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_civilian_deaths = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_civilian_death_request }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_civilian_deaths = random_terror_civilian_death_request }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_context_type = constant:condemnation_context.unspecified }
	random_terror_add_condemnation_source = yes
}

random_terror_add_hidden_government_coverup_condemnation = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_atrocity_or_coverup
	set_country_flag = random_terror_government_coverup_condemnation_recorded
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_source_type = constant:condemnation_source.coverup }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_base_gain = constant:random_terror_tuning.government_coverup_condemnation_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_visibility_type = constant:condemnation_visibility.hidden }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_severity_type = constant:condemnation_severity.major }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_civilian_deaths = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_context_type = constant:condemnation_context.concealment_operation }
	random_terror_add_condemnation_source = yes
}

random_terror_expose_event31_coverup_condemnation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = random_terror_government_coverup_condemnation_recorded
			check_variable = { condemnation_hidden_coverup > constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { condemnation_source_type = constant:condemnation_source.coverup }
		set_temp_variable = { condemnation_hidden_exposure_fraction = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		set_temp_variable = { condemnation_context_type = constant:condemnation_context.observer_report }
		condemnation_expose_hidden_bucket = yes
		set_country_flag = random_terror_government_coverup_exposed
	}
}

	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_context_type = constant:condemnation_context.black_market_exposure }
	random_terror_add_condemnation_source = yes

random_terror_add_public_forced_rule_condemnation = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_forced_rule_condemnation_recorded } }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_forced_rule_condemnation_recorded
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_source_type = constant:condemnation_source.atrocity }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_base_gain = constant:random_terror_tuning.forced_rule_condemnation_gain }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_visibility_type = constant:condemnation_visibility.public }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_severity_type = constant:condemnation_severity.mass_death }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_civilian_deaths = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_context_type = constant:condemnation_context.unspecified }
		random_terror_add_condemnation_source = yes
	}
}

random_terror_clamp_country_values = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = random_terror_pressure
		min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum
		max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = random_terror_response_legitimacy
		min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum
		max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope.  Input: caller-owned temp random_terror_pending_pressure_delta,
# consumed and reset to zero.
random_terror_apply_pressure = {
	random_terror_initialize_country_state = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_pressure = random_terror_pending_pressure_delta }
	random_terror_clamp_country_values = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
}

# COUNTRY scope.  Input: caller-owned temp random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta,
# consumed and reset to zero.
random_terror_apply_legitimacy = {
	random_terror_initialize_country_state = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_response_legitimacy = random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta }
	random_terror_clamp_country_values = yes
	random_terror_achievement_record_legitimacy = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
}

random_terror_update_state_stage = {
	random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_previous_state_stage = random_terror_state_stage }
	set_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.clear }
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_dormant
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_active
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_entrenched
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.dormant }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_dormant
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_active_threshold } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.active }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_active
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_entrenched_threshold } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.entrenched }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_entrenched
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_armed_threshold } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.armed_insurgency }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_value.minimum_pressure_for_territory }
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.minimum_pressure_for_territory }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_reinforced }
		}
		set_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.lost_local_control }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = random_terror_state_active
				has_state_flag = random_terror_state_entrenched
				has_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency
				has_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
			}
			NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_crisis_counted }
		}
		set_state_flag = random_terror_crisis_counted
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_crisis_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	# A state that newly becomes an active crisis is a new hotspot for its
	# owner; containment and corridor missions measure spread with this count.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_previous_state_stage < constant:random_terror_state_stage.active }
			check_variable = { var = random_terror_state_stage value = constant:random_terror_state_stage.active compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		OWNER = { add_to_variable = { random_terror_hotspot_count = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
	}
	# Recovery (Part 1): a state that falls back below Active keeps its
	# recovery marker until it is Clear, so the government can still reach
	# its residual cells and the crisis cannot close around them.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = random_terror_previous_state_stage value = constant:random_terror_state_stage.active compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage < constant:random_terror_state_stage.active }
		}
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.clear }
		}
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery
	}
	# A Clear state has no cell left to carry a copycat wave, a training area
	# or scattered survivors into its neighbours.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.clear } }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_copycat_recorded
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_training_area_recorded
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_survivors_displaced
	}
	random_terror_refresh_state_modifier = yes
	# A stage change can move the most severe crisis of the owner or controller.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = random_terror_state_stage value = random_terror_previous_state_stage compare = equals } } }
		OWNER = { random_terror_refresh_priority_state = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = OWNER } }
			CONTROLLER = { random_terror_refresh_priority_state = yes }
		}
	}
	random_terror_achievement_record_state_stage = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope: a government.  Marks its most severe crisis state, among the
# states it controls and its own cores held by others, as its priority crisis
# state (random_terror_priority_state, state flag
# random_terror_priority_state_<tag>).  Higher stage wins, then higher
# activity.  Generic government decisions target only this state, so each
# offers one row.
random_terror_refresh_priority_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_priority_state }
		var:random_terror_priority_state = { clr_state_flag = random_terror_priority_state_@PREV }
		clear_variable = random_terror_priority_state
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_priority_best_stage = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_priority_best_activity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	# The response category header shows how many crisis states it holds.
	set_variable = { random_terror_active_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = { random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes }
		PREV = { add_to_variable = { random_terror_active_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
		random_terror_consider_priority_state = yes
	}
	every_core_state = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes
			NOT = { is_controlled_by = PREV }
		}
		random_terror_consider_priority_state = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_priority_best_stage > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_priority_state = random_terror_priority_candidate }
		var:random_terror_priority_state = { set_state_flag = random_terror_priority_state_@PREV }
	}
	# With no active crisis left, a recovering state it controls becomes the
	# priority state, the one with the most residual cells first, so Support
	# Victims keeps one row through the recovery phase.
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery } }
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_priority_candidate = THIS }
		}
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery }
			random_terror_consider_priority_state = yes
		}
		set_variable = { random_terror_priority_state = random_terror_priority_candidate }
		var:random_terror_priority_state = { set_state_flag = random_terror_priority_state_@PREV }
	}
}

# STATE scope: one candidate for random_terror_refresh_priority_state.
random_terror_consider_priority_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage > random_terror_priority_best_stage }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = random_terror_state_stage value = random_terror_priority_best_stage compare = equals }
					check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > random_terror_priority_best_activity }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_priority_best_stage = random_terror_state_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_priority_best_activity = random_terror_state_activity }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_priority_candidate = THIS }
	}
}

# STATE scope.  Keeps exactly one staged Event 31 modifier on a crisis state,
# the one matching random_terror_state_stage, and none on a Clear state.
random_terror_refresh_state_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_dormant }
			NOT = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.dormant } }
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_dormant }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_active }
			NOT = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.active } }
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_active }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_entrenched }
			NOT = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.entrenched } }
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_entrenched }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency }
			NOT = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.armed_insurgency } }
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_lost_local_control }
			NOT = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.lost_local_control } }
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_lost_local_control }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.dormant }
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_dormant } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_dormant }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.active }
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_active } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_active }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.entrenched }
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_entrenched } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_entrenched }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.armed_insurgency }
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.lost_local_control }
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_lost_local_control } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_state_lost_local_control }
	}
}

# STATE scope.  Input: caller-owned temp random_terror_pending_activity_delta,
# consumed and reset to zero.
random_terror_add_state_activity = {
	random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = random_terror_pending_activity_delta }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = random_terror_state_activity
		min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum
		max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
}

random_terror_mark_state_cooldown = {
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_cooldown_days }
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
	set_variable = { random_terror_state_cooldown_until = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_cooldown_until = chaosx_date_offset }
}

random_terror_record_chaos = {
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_pressure_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason = constant:chaos_meter_history_reason.generic.increase }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason_custom = 1 }
	add_chaos_meter_value = yes
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Incident engine and global pacing
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = {
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_requested = random_terror_civilian_death_request }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_minimum_remaining = constant:random_terror_tuning.minimum_remaining_population }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.civilian_combat }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_log_deaths = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_target_country = OWNER }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_has_target_country = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_contract_supplied = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_applied = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_result = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	apply_exact_state_civilian_population_loss = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_applied > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		OWNER = { set_country_flag = random_terror_cycle_civilian_loss }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_applied > constant:random_terror_tuning.achievement_major_loss_deaths } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_major_civilian_loss_recorded
		OWNER = {
			set_country_flag = random_terror_major_civilian_loss_recorded
			add_to_variable = { random_terror_major_civilian_losses = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
		}
	}
}

random_terror_apply_incident = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
	# The incident report names this state and any measured civilian loss.
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_report_state_id = THIS }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_report_loss = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	# Caller-owned storage for the loss the exact state-loss helpers return.
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_applied = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_unlocked }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_transnational_link_active
	}
	save_event_target_as = random_terror_current_state
	random_terror_mark_state_cooldown = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_incident_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.civilian_attack }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_civilian_attack = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.civilian_attack }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_transport_disruption = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.transport_disruption }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_depot_sabotage = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.depot_sabotage }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_port_convoy_attack = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.port_convoy_attack }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_official_assassination_attempt = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.official_assassination_attempt }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_hostage_crisis = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.hostage_crisis }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_security_site_attack = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.security_site_attack }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_propaganda_surge = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.propaganda_surge }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_arms_theft = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.arms_theft }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_sponsor_evidence = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.sponsor_evidence }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_smuggling_corridor = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.smuggling_corridor }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_training_area = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.training_area }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_copycat = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.copycat }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_failed_raid = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.failed_raid }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_intelligence_breakthrough = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.intelligence_breakthrough }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_defection = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.defection }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_relief_strain = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.relief_strain }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_military_defection = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.military_defection }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_capital_infiltration = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.capital_infiltration }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_rival_organization = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.rival_organization }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_community_rejection = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.community_rejection }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_joint_operation = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.joint_operation }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_sponsor_exposure = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.sponsor_exposure }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_cannibal_clash = constant:random_terror_incident_weight.cannibal_clash }
	# Event 14 rivalry: a clash with cannibal warbands needs one next door;
	# otherwise its weight goes to a clash between rival organizations.
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_neighbor_state = {
					CONTROLLER = { is_cannibalism_country = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_rival_organization = random_terror_draw_incident_cannibal_clash }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_incident_cannibal_clash = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	random_list = {
		random_terror_draw_incident_civilian_attack = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.civilian_attack } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_transport_disruption = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.transport_disruption } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_depot_sabotage = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.depot_sabotage } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_port_convoy_attack = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.port_convoy_attack } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_official_assassination_attempt = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.official_assassination_attempt } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_hostage_crisis = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.hostage_crisis } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_security_site_attack = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.security_site_attack } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_propaganda_surge = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.propaganda_surge } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_arms_theft = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.arms_theft } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_sponsor_evidence = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.sponsor_evidence } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_smuggling_corridor = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.smuggling_corridor } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_training_area = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.training_area } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_copycat = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.copycat } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_failed_raid = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.failed_raid } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_intelligence_breakthrough = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.intelligence_breakthrough } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_defection = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.defection } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_relief_strain = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.relief_strain } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_military_defection = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.military_defection } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_capital_infiltration = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.capital_infiltration } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_rival_organization = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.rival_organization } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_community_rejection = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.community_rejection } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_joint_operation = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.joint_operation } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_sponsor_exposure = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.sponsor_exposure } }
		random_terror_draw_incident_cannibal_clash = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.cannibal_clash } }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_pressure_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_tuning.civilian_deaths_minor }
	# Normalize the selected family to the exact state context.  Invalid naval,
	# rail, capital, or military-defection outcomes become a compatible civic
	# incident instead of silently applying an unrelated effect.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.port_convoy_attack }
			NOT = { is_coastal = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.civilian_attack }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.transport_disruption }
			NOT = { infrastructure > constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.civilian_attack }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.capital_infiltration }
			NOT = { is_capital = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.security_site_attack }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.military_defection }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.defection }
	}
	# Guards on a protected transport state can turn a route attack into an
	# attack on themselves.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.transport_disruption }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.port_convoy_attack }
			}
			random_terror_state_transport_protected = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_transport_foiled = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.transport_protection_foiled }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_transport_breached = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.transport_protection_breached }
		random_list = {
			random_terror_draw_transport_foiled = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.security_site_attack } }
			random_terror_draw_transport_breached = { }
		}
	}
	# Protective cordons (first emphasis) guard crowds and routes across the
	# country: at the same odds an attack on them strikes the guards instead.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.civilian_attack }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.transport_disruption }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.port_convoy_attack }
			}
			NOT = { random_terror_state_transport_protected = yes }
			CONTROLLER = { has_country_flag = random_terror_emphasis_protection }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_cordon_foiled = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.transport_protection_foiled }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_cordon_breached = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.transport_protection_breached }
		random_list = {
			random_terror_draw_cordon_foiled = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.security_site_attack } }
			random_terror_draw_cordon_breached = { }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.civilian_attack }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.hostage_crisis }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.security_site_attack }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_pressure_gain }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_tuning.civilian_deaths_major }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.transport_disruption } }
		set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:infrastructure }
		set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_amount = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_building_damage }
		damage_state_building_dynamic = yes
		set_state_flag = random_terror_transport_disrupted
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_disruption_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.depot_sabotage } }
		set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:arms_factory }
		set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_amount = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_building_damage }
		damage_state_building_dynamic = yes
		set_state_flag = random_terror_depot_compromised
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.port_convoy_attack } }
		if = {
			limit = { is_coastal = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:naval_base }
			set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_amount = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_building_damage }
			damage_state_building_dynamic = yes
			set_state_flag = random_terror_port_disrupted
			add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_disruption_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.arms_theft } }
		set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:arms_factory }
		set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_amount = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_building_damage }
		damage_state_building_dynamic = yes
		set_state_flag = random_terror_arms_theft_recorded
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.sponsor_evidence }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.sponsor_exposure }
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = random_terror_sponsor_evidence_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
		CONTROLLER = { set_country_flag = random_terror_quiet_leverage_available }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.smuggling_corridor } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.training_area } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_training_area_recorded
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.copycat } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_copycat_recorded
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_pressure_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.failed_raid } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_failed_raid_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.readiness_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.intelligence_breakthrough }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.joint_operation }
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = random_terror_intelligence_breakthrough_recorded
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_loss }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.defection }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.military_defection }
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = random_terror_defection_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.relief_strain } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_relief_strain_recorded
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_pressure_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.capital_infiltration } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_capital_infiltration_recorded
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_pressure_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.hostage_crisis } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_crisis_recorded
		CONTROLLER = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_1 }
					random_terror_can_hold_response_mission = yes
				}
				event_target:random_terror_current_state = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_mission_subject }
				random_terror_begin_hostage_mission = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.rival_organization } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_rival_organization_recorded
		subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.global_unity_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.community_rejection } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_community_rejection_recorded
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked }
			set_state_flag = random_terror_muslim_community_opposition_recorded
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.global_unity_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.cannibal_clash } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_cannibal_clash_recorded
		subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
		random_terror_resolve_cannibal_clash = yes
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_report_loss = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.propaganda_surge } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_propaganda_surge_recorded
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_active_threshold }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.civilian_attack }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.hostage_crisis }
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.security_site_attack }
			}
		}
		random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = yes
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_report_loss = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = random_terror_incident_pressure_delta }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = random_terror_state_pressure
		min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum
		max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
	# Opening shock (Part 1): a Clear or Dormant state enters Active, while an
	# established cell advances by the ordinary incident gain.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity < constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_opening } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_opening }
		# A distant demonstration attack (caller-owned temp input
		# random_terror_incident_trace_only = 1) leaves only Dormant traces
		# unless the attack itself recorded a reason for a cell to stay.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_trace_only = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				NOT = { random_terror_state_has_recorded_spread_reason = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_spread.trace_activity }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = random_terror_state_activity }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_pending_activity_delta min = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	random_terror_add_state_activity = yes
	set_variable = { random_terror_last_incident_type = random_terror_incident_type }
	CONTROLLER = {
		random_terror_initialize_country_state = yes
		random_terror_mark_affected_country = yes
		random_terror_schedule_recovery_check = yes
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_cycle_incident_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_report_incident_type = random_terror_incident_type }
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_report_state = random_terror_report_state_id }
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_report_loss = random_terror_report_loss }
		# A spread or distant attack records its origin after this returns.
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_report_spread_reason = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.none }
		clear_variable = random_terror_last_report_origin
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = random_terror_incident_pressure_delta }
		random_terror_apply_pressure = yes
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_loss }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = -1 }
		random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_incident_type = constant:random_terror_incident.capital_infiltration }
				check_variable = { random_terror_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.high }
				event_target:random_terror_current_state = {
					is_capital = yes
					check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_armed_threshold }
				}
				check_variable = { random_terror_response_legitimacy < constant:random_terror_value.low }
			}
			random_terror_trigger_coup_attempt = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_incident_count = 1 }
		random_terror_achievement_record_incident = yes
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_memory_duration = constant:random_terror_tuning.incident_memory_days }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_recent_incident days = [MEMORY_DAYS] } }
			MEMORY_DAYS = "[?random_terror_incident_memory_duration|.0]"
		}
		# One report per government at a time: a second incident before the
		# queued report opens (a pulse incident followed by a spread into a
		# new state) is told by that report, which reads the latest incident.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_report_queued } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_report_queued
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.2 days = 1 }
		}
	}
	# Re-read the stage before the controller refreshes its priority crisis
	# state and schedules its next pulse.
	random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	CONTROLLER = {
		random_terror_refresh_priority_state = yes
		random_terror_schedule_country_pulse = yes
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_total_incidents = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_automatic_wave_active }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_last_wave_affected_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_record_chaos = yes
	random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_stage = constant:random_terror_state_stage.lost_local_control }
		}
		random_terror_try_spawn_actor = yes
		# A capital cannot secede.  Its government faces a coup attempt instead,
		# and the civil-war check below carries any other blocked state.
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				is_capital = yes
				is_controlled_by = OWNER
			}
			OWNER = { random_terror_trigger_coup_attempt = yes }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_armed_threshold }
			CONTROLLER = { check_variable = { random_terror_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.minimum_pressure_for_uprising } }
			CONTROLLER = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_civil_war_started } }
		}
		CONTROLLER = { random_terror_start_pressure_civil_war = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: an affected government.  Keeps at most one pending recurring
# incident pulse while the country has an active crisis state.  The delay
# comes from the Part 2 band of its most severe state, then a random spread
# keeps neighbouring crises from firing in lockstep.  A timed guard flag
# outlives the event slightly so a lost pulse can never block the next one.
random_terror_schedule_country_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_pulse_pending }
			random_terror_country_can_receive_pulse = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pulse_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_incident_pulse_days }
		set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = random_terror_pulse_spread
			min = constant:random_terror_tuning.pulse_spread_min
			max = constant:random_terror_tuning.pulse_spread_max
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pulse_delay_days = random_terror_pulse_spread }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = random_terror_pulse_delay_days
			min = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.pulse_minimum
			max = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.pulse_maximum
		}
		round_temp_variable = random_terror_pulse_delay_days
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pulse_guard_days = random_terror_pulse_delay_days }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_pulse_guard_days = constant:random_terror_tuning.pulse_flag_buffer_days }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_pulse_pending days = [GUARD_DAYS] } }
			GUARD_DAYS = "[?random_terror_pulse_guard_days|.0]"
		}
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.96 days = random_terror_pulse_delay_days }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the hidden pulse event.  Applies one incident through the
# ordinary incident engine to a valid active state, respecting the state
# cooldown, then reschedules only while the crisis remains active.  It never
# touches repeatable weight, minor-event pacing or the Event Logs history.
random_terror_run_country_pulse = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_pulse_pending
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_country_can_receive_pulse = yes }
		random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes
				random_terror_state_is_eligible_incident_site = yes
			}
			random_terror_apply_incident = yes
		}
		# The same pulse may let a cell move into one neighbouring state.
		random_terror_try_spread = yes
		# A pulse with no eligible site is postponed rather than redirected.
		random_terror_schedule_country_pulse = yes
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cross-state spread, survivor displacement, cross-border spread and distant
# demonstration attacks (Part 2 Cross-state spread and Cross-border spread,
# Part 4 Evolution I survivor displacement and Evolution II distant attacks).
# Everything runs inside the event-owned country pulse (chaosx.nr31.96), so
# there is no periodic world scan and no change to repeatable weight or
# minor-event pacing.  A government opens at most one new hotspot per spread
# window; every new hotspot goes through random_terror_apply_incident, so the
# stage, report, hotspot count, recovery check and pulse all follow.
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope, ROOT = THIS = the government whose pulse is running.  Rolls
# one random_list: open a hotspot next to a spread source, launch a distant
# demonstration attack (Evolution II, high Network Reach, global window
# open), or hold.  Active containment or transport-protection missions and
# a Security Surge halve the odds; Temporary Movement Restrictions block it.
random_terror_try_spread = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_country_spread_window_open = yes
			OR = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					random_terror_state_is_spread_source = yes
					any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_spread_target = yes }
				}
				random_terror_distant_attack_possible = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_open = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_distant = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_hold = constant:random_terror_spread.hold_weight }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					random_terror_state_is_spread_source = yes
					any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_spread_target = yes }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_open = constant:random_terror_spread.open_weight }
			# Scattered survivors are already on the move.
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_controlled_state = {
						has_state_flag = random_terror_survivors_displaced
						random_terror_state_is_spread_source = yes
						any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_spread_target = yes }
					}
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_open = constant:random_terror_spread.survivors_factor }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_1_unlocked }
				multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_open = constant:random_terror_spread.organized_factor }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_containment }
				multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_open = constant:random_terror_spread.mission_factor }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_transport }
				multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_open = constant:random_terror_spread.mission_factor }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_security_surge_active }
				multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_open = constant:random_terror_spread.surge_factor }
			}
			round_temp_variable = random_terror_draw_spread_open
		}
		if = {
			limit = { random_terror_distant_attack_possible = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_distant = constant:random_terror_spread.distant_weight }
		}
		random_list = {
			random_terror_draw_spread_open = { random_terror_spread_to_neighbor = yes }
			random_terror_draw_spread_distant = { random_terror_launch_distant_attack = yes }
			random_terror_draw_spread_hold = { }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope, ROOT = THIS = the spreading government.  Picks one source,
# scattered survivors first, then one neighbouring Clear or Dormant target:
# its own state, or after Evolution II a state of another government.  The
# target receives an ordinary incident, and its report names the source
# state and the reason the cell moved.
random_terror_spread_to_neighbor = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_source_found = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				has_state_flag = random_terror_survivors_displaced
				random_terror_state_is_spread_source = yes
				any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_spread_target = yes }
			}
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = random_terror_survivors_displaced
				random_terror_state_is_spread_source = yes
				any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_spread_target = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_spread_source
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_source_found = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				random_terror_state_is_spread_source = yes
				any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_spread_target = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_spread_source
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_source_found = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_spread_source_found = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
		event_target:random_terror_spread_source = {
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_origin_state = THIS }
			# The report names the strongest reason the cell moved.
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_reason_code = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.actor }
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_copycat_recorded }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_reason_code = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.copycat }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_training_area_recorded }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_reason_code = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.training }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_port_disrupted }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_reason_code = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.port }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_transport_disrupted }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_reason_code = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.transport }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_recorded }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_reason_code = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.corridor }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_survivors_displaced }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spread_reason_code = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.survivors }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_domestic = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_foreign = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_domestic_spread_target = yes } }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_domestic = constant:random_terror_spread.domestic_weight }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { any_neighbor_state = { random_terror_state_is_foreign_spread_target = yes } }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_spread_foreign = constant:random_terror_spread.foreign_weight }
			}
			random_list = {
				random_terror_draw_spread_domestic = {
					random_neighbor_state = {
						limit = { random_terror_state_is_domestic_spread_target = yes }
						random_terror_open_spread_hotspot = yes
					}
				}
				random_terror_draw_spread_foreign = {
					random_neighbor_state = {
						limit = { random_terror_state_is_foreign_spread_target = yes }
						random_terror_open_spread_hotspot = yes
					}
				}
			}
			# The scattered survivors have found a new base.
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_survivors_displaced
		}
		random_terror_mark_spread_window = yes
	}
}

# STATE scope: the new hotspot.  Inputs (caller-owned temps):
# random_terror_spread_origin_state and random_terror_spread_reason_code.
# Applies an ordinary incident, then records the origin for its report.
random_terror_open_spread_hotspot = {
	random_terror_apply_incident = yes
	CONTROLLER = {
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_report_spread_reason = random_terror_spread_reason_code }
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_report_origin = random_terror_spread_origin_state }
	}
	# Cross-border spread makes the source country a safe haven (Part 4
	# Evolution II) that the receiving government can press.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT } }
		ROOT = { random_terror_mark_safe_haven = yes }
		CONTROLLER = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope, ROOT = THIS = the government whose pulse launches it.  A
# distant demonstration attack (Part 4 Evolution II) on a country with no
# active cell.  It costs Network Reach, closes a global window, and leaves
# only Dormant traces unless the attack itself records a reason for the cell
# to stay (random_terror_incident_trace_only in random_terror_apply_incident).
# Its single country selection runs only after the rare roll succeeds.
random_terror_launch_distant_attack = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_distant_applied = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	random_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_country_is_distant_attack_target = yes }
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				random_terror_state_is_eligible_incident_site = yes
				NOT = { random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_trace_only = constant:random_terror_value.one }
			random_terror_apply_incident = yes
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_incident_trace_only = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			CONTROLLER = {
				set_variable = { random_terror_last_report_spread_reason = constant:random_terror_spread_reason.distant }
				clear_variable = random_terror_last_report_origin
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_distant_applied = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_distant_applied = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_spread.distant_reach_cost }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:random_terror_spread.distant_window_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_distant_ready_date = global.date }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_distant_ready_date = chaosx_date_offset }
		random_terror_mark_spread_window = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: a government that has just opened a new hotspot.  Closes its
# spread window for window_days, or high_reach_window_days at high Network
# Reach.
random_terror_mark_spread_window = {
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:random_terror_spread.window_days }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach > constant:random_terror_value.high } }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:random_terror_spread.high_reach_window_days }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
	chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
	set_variable = { random_terror_spread_ready_date = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_spread_ready_date = chaosx_date_offset }
}

# STATE scope: a cell that a partial or costly raid, or a Security Surge
# without an intelligence breakthrough, has scattered.  Its survivors can
# carry it into a neighbouring state for survivors_days.
random_terror_mark_survivors_displaced = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_survivors_duration = constant:random_terror_spread.survivors_days }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_state_flag = { flag = random_terror_survivors_displaced days = [SURVIVOR_DAYS] } }
		SURVIVOR_DAYS = "[?random_terror_survivors_duration|.0]"
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the government choosing its first emphasis in the opening
# incident report of a crisis (chaosx.nr31.2).  Each choice lasts
# random_terror_emphasis.days, adds its timed country modifier and a timed
# flag read by the matching decision family, and acts at once on the
# reported state (random_terror_last_report_state).  Nothing locks a doctrine:
# every response stays available, and the emphasis simply ends.  Each
# choose effect owns its random_terror_emphasis_duration temp variable.
random_terror_emphasis_begin = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_emphasis_chosen
}

random_terror_choose_emphasis_protection = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_emphasis_duration = constant:random_terror_emphasis.days }
	random_terror_emphasis_begin = yes
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_emphasis_protection days = random_terror_emphasis_duration }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_emphasis_protection days = [EMPHASIS_DAYS] } }
		EMPHASIS_DAYS = "[?random_terror_emphasis_duration|.0]"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_last_report_state }
		var:random_terror_last_report_state = {
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
}

random_terror_choose_emphasis_intelligence = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_emphasis_duration = constant:random_terror_emphasis.days }
	random_terror_emphasis_begin = yes
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_emphasis_intelligence days = random_terror_emphasis_duration }
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_emphasis_intelligence days = [EMPHASIS_DAYS] }
			set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_emphasis_first_raid days = [EMPHASIS_DAYS] }
		}
		EMPHASIS_DAYS = "[?random_terror_emphasis_duration|.0]"
	}
}

random_terror_choose_emphasis_victims = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_emphasis_duration = constant:random_terror_emphasis.days }
	random_terror_emphasis_begin = yes
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_emphasis_victims days = random_terror_emphasis_duration }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_emphasis_victims days = [EMPHASIS_DAYS] } }
		EMPHASIS_DAYS = "[?random_terror_emphasis_duration|.0]"
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_gain }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
}

random_terror_choose_emphasis_coercion = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_emphasis_duration = constant:random_terror_emphasis.days }
	random_terror_emphasis_begin = yes
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_emphasis_coercion days = random_terror_emphasis_duration }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_emphasis_coercion days = [EMPHASIS_DAYS] } }
		EMPHASIS_DAYS = "[?random_terror_emphasis_duration|.0]"
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_emphasis.coercion_legitimacy_loss }
	multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_last_report_state }
		var:random_terror_last_report_state = {
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_loss }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
			random_terror_add_state_activity = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: an affected government.  Keeps exactly one pending hidden
# recovery check (chaosx.nr31.97) for as long as the crisis stays open, so
# the recovery phase and the closure never depend on a stage change being
# observed.  A timed guard flag outlives the event slightly.
random_terror_schedule_recovery_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = random_terror_affected_country
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_actor }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_recovery_check_pending }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_recovery_check_days = constant:random_terror_recovery.check_days }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_recovery_guard_days = random_terror_recovery_check_days }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_recovery_guard_days = constant:random_terror_tuning.pulse_flag_buffer_days }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_recovery_check_pending days = [GUARD_DAYS] } }
			GUARD_DAYS = "[?random_terror_recovery_guard_days|.0]"
		}
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.97 days = random_terror_recovery_check_days }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the hidden recovery check.  In the recovery phase fear fades
# and residual cells lose local support while no attack lands: Terror
# Pressure and the activity of every recovering state fall, and the crisis
# closes once its recovery obligations end.  The check reschedules itself
# while the crisis stays open, in any phase.
random_terror_run_recovery_check = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_recovery_check_pending
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_country_in_recovery = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_recovery.pressure_decay }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
		random_terror_apply_pressure = yes
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_recovery.activity_decay }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
			random_terror_add_state_activity = yes
		}
		random_terror_close_country_recovery = yes
	}
	random_terror_schedule_recovery_check = yes
}

random_terror_identify_highest_enabled_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_organized_cells_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_transnational_network_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_territorial_insurgency_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_3 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_jihadist_international_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_4 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_final_jihad_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_5 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
	}
}

random_terror_apply_evolution_stage = {
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = random_terror_resolved_evolution }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_1_unlocked
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = random_terror_resolved_evolution value = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_unlocked
		every_state = {
			limit = { random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes }
			set_state_flag = random_terror_transnational_link_active
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = random_terror_resolved_evolution value = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_unlocked
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = random_terror_resolved_evolution value = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad } }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_5_unlocked
		set_global_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_active
		set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_enabled
		random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
	}
}

random_terror_schedule_next_evolution = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_candidate_pending }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = world_end }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage < constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_organized_cells_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_1_unlocked
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_1 }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_2 }
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_transnational_network_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_unlocked
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_2 }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_3 }
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_territorial_insurgency_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
