			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_unlocked
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_3 }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_4 }
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_jihadist_international_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_4 }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_5 }
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_final_jihad_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_evolution_organized_cells_days }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network } } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_evolution_transnational_network_days } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency } } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_evolution_territorial_insurgency_days } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international } } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_evolution_jihadist_international_days } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_evolution_candidate = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad } } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_evolution_final_jihad_days } }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_evolution_delay_days min = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.minimum max = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.maximum }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_candidate_stage = random_terror_evolution_candidate }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = random_terror_evolution_delay_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_candidate_due_date = global.date }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_candidate_due_date = chaosx_date_offset }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_candidate_pending
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.92 days = random_terror_evolution_delay_days }
		}
	}
}

random_terror_process_evolution_candidate = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = global.random_terror_evolution_candidate_stage }
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_candidate_pending
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_evolution_candidate_stage
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_evolution_candidate_due_date
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_transition_authorized = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage < constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_organized_cells_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_transition_authorized = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
			has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_1_unlocked
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_1 }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_2 }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_transnational_network_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_transition_authorized = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
			has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_unlocked
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_2 }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_3 }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_territorial_insurgency_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_transition_authorized = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
			has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_unlocked
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_3 }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_4 }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_jihadist_international_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_transition_authorized = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
			has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_4 }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = events_log_disabled_evolution_31_31_5 }
			check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = constant:random_terror_value.evolution_final_jihad_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_evolution_transition_authorized = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_evolution_transition_authorized = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
		random_terror_apply_evolution_stage = yes
		random_terror_record_evolution = yes
		random_terror_schedule_next_evolution = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_candidate_lapsed
	}
}

random_terror_resolve_evolution = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolved_opening_applied }
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_total_incidents < constant:random_terror_value.one }
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage < constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		random_terror_identify_highest_enabled_evolution = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_resolved_evolution > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
			random_terror_apply_evolution_stage = yes
			random_terror_record_evolution = yes
		}
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolved_opening_applied
	}
	else = {
		random_terror_schedule_next_evolution = yes
	}
}

random_terror_record_evolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:random_terror_identity.event_id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:random_terror_identity.evolution_type }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = global.random_terror_evolution_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = global.random_terror_evolution_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 0 }
	record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
	random_terror_emit_evolution_report = yes
}

# One presentation per evolution, each guarded by a one-time global flag.
# Global Jihad setup records its evolutions in the Event Logs but holds these
# popups and news back in favour of its own launch reports; the guard flags
# are still set so nothing fires late.
#   II  report chaosx.nr31.3 and news 311
#   III report chaosx.nr31.4, deferred until the first territorial actor exists
#   IV  report chaosx.nr31.5
#   V   news 313
random_terror_emit_evolution_report = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_report_sent }
		}
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_report_sent
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress } }
			every_country = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.3 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_network_news_sent } }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_network_news_sent
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress } }
				news_event = { id = chaosx.news.311 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_report_sent }
		}
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_report_sent
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_territorial_actor_exists }
				every_country = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.4 } }
			}
			else = {
				set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_report_deferred
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_report_sent }
		}
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_report_sent
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress } }
			every_country = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.5 } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_news_sent }
		}
		set_global_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_news_sent
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress } }
			news_event = { id = chaosx.news.313 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# The first territorial actor of the campaign: world news 312 once, and the
# Territorial Insurgency report when that evolution arrived before any actor.
random_terror_emit_first_actor_news = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_first_actor_news_sent } }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_first_actor_news_sent
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress } }
			news_event = { id = chaosx.news.312 days = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_report_deferred }
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_report_deferred
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress } }
			every_country = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.4 days = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

random_terror_resolve_wave_target_count = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.baseline_targets }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_1_unlocked } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.organized_targets } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_unlocked } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.transnational_targets } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_unlocked } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.territorial_targets } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.jihadist_targets } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_5_unlocked } set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.final_targets } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_scenario_launched
			has_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_wave_target_count
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = global.random_terror_scenario_wave_target_count }
	}
}

random_terror_add_current_country_to_wave_target_pool = {
	set_temp_variable = { individual_crisis_candidate_base_weight = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	set_temp_variable = { individual_crisis_candidate_adjusted_weight = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_country_has_active_package = yes }
		adjust_individual_crisis_existing_provider_candidate_ticket_weight = yes
	}
	else = {
		adjust_individual_crisis_candidate_ticket_weight = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_pool_remaining = individual_crisis_candidate_adjusted_weight }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_wave_pool_remaining > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		add_to_temp_array = { random_terror_wave_country_pool = THIS }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_pool_remaining = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
}

random_terror_select_and_apply_wave_target = {
	# Allocate caller-owned storage before nested candidate helpers return.
	add_to_temp_array = { random_terror_wave_country_pool = THIS }
	clear_temp_array = random_terror_wave_country_pool
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_site_applied = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	every_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_country_is_eligible_wave_target = yes }
		random_terror_add_current_country_to_wave_target_pool = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_wave_country_pool^num > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = random_terror_wave_country_pool
			limit = { random_terror_country_is_eligible_wave_target = yes }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { random_terror_state_is_eligible_incident_site = yes }
				random_terror_apply_incident = yes
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_site_applied = constant:random_terror_value.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_wave_site_applied > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_target_reservation_duration = constant:random_terror_tuning.wave_cooldown_days }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_target_reserved days = [RESERVATION_DAYS] } }
					RESERVATION_DAYS = "[?random_terror_target_reservation_duration|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = random_terror_wave_country_pool
}

random_terror_run_global_wave = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
	random_terror_resolve_evolution = yes
	# Own the output in this caller before the nested resolver modifies it.
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_wave_target_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.baseline_targets }
	random_terror_resolve_wave_target_count = yes
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_last_wave_affected_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_global_flag = random_terror_wave_recorded
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = random_terror_wave_target_count
		value = random_terror_wave_index
		random_terror_select_and_apply_wave_target = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global Jihad triggerable scenario
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Reads the requested pattern and intensity and writes the scenario targets.
# It sets no evolution flag or stage: those follow only a passed preflight, so
# a blocked launch leaves the world unchanged (Part 7 acceptance case 11).
random_terror_resolve_scenario_profile = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_global_jihad_requested_type }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_type = random_terror_global_jihad_requested_type }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.random_pattern }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_global_jihad_requested_intensity }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_intensity = random_terror_global_jihad_requested_intensity }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = triggerable_scenarios_intensity }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_intensity = triggerable_scenarios_intensity }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_intensity = constant:random_terror_intensity.medium }
	}
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_global_jihad_type = random_terror_scenario_input_type }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_global_jihad_scenario_intensity = random_terror_scenario_input_intensity }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = random_terror_scenario_input_type }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.random_pattern } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_scenario_pattern = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.scenario_pattern }
		random_list = {
			random_terror_draw_runtime_scenario_pattern = { set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.dispersed_networks } }
			random_terror_draw_runtime_scenario_pattern = { set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.border_corridors } }
			random_terror_draw_runtime_scenario_pattern = { set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.capital_uprisings } }
			random_terror_draw_runtime_scenario_pattern = { set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.territorial_fronts } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_intensity = constant:random_terror_intensity.low } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_country_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_targets }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_state_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_states }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_actor_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_actors }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_pressure }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_activity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_network_reach }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_unity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_readiness }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_legitimacy = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_legitimacy }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_response_capacity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_civil_war_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_low_civil_war_target }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_intensity = constant:random_terror_intensity.high } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_country_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_targets }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_state_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_states }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_actor_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_actors }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_pressure }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_activity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_network_reach }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_unity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_readiness }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_legitimacy = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_legitimacy }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_response_capacity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_civil_war_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_high_civil_war_target }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_intensity = constant:random_terror_intensity.maximum } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_country_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_targets }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_state_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_states }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_actor_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_actors }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_pressure }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_activity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_network_reach }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_unity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_readiness }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_legitimacy = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_legitimacy }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_response_capacity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_civil_war_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_maximum_civil_war_target }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_country_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_targets }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_state_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_states }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_actor_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_actors }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_pressure }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_activity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_network_reach }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_unity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_readiness }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_legitimacy = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_legitimacy }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_response_capacity }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_civil_war_target = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_medium_civil_war_target }
	}
	# The evolution stage this launch will open.  The preflight counts only the
	# carriers whose evolution gate this stage unlocks.
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_input_intensity = constant:random_terror_intensity.maximum } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage > global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage = global.random_terror_evolution_stage }
	}
}

# Runs only after a passed preflight.  Opens the scenario's evolutions without
# ever lowering a stage the ordinary progression already reached.
random_terror_apply_scenario_evolutions = {
	set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_1_unlocked
	set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_unlocked
	set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_unlocked
	set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage < constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad } }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_5_unlocked
		set_global_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_active
		set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_enabled
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
		random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
	}
}

# A blocked launch removes the profile it wrote, leaving no scenario state.
random_terror_clear_scenario_profile = {
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_global_jihad_type
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_global_jihad_scenario_intensity
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_country_target
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_state_target
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_actor_target
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_pressure
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_activity
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_network_reach
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_unity
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_readiness
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_legitimacy
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_response_capacity
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_civil_war_target
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_existing_actor_count
}

random_terror_record_scenario_evolutions = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_final_evolution = global.random_terror_evolution_stage }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_1_recorded } }
		random_terror_record_evolution = yes
		set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_1_recorded
	}
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_2_recorded } }
		random_terror_record_evolution = yes
		set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_2_recorded
	}
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_3_recorded } }
		random_terror_record_evolution = yes
		set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_3_recorded
	}
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_4_recorded } }
		random_terror_record_evolution = yes
		set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_4_recorded
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_final_evolution = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_evolution_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_5_recorded } }
			random_terror_record_evolution = yes
			set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_evolution_5_recorded
		}
	}
}

random_terror_scenario_preflight = {
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_ready = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_reason = constant:random_terror_scenario_reject_reason.none }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_valid_country_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_valid_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_existing_actor_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	every_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_scenario_candidate_country = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_valid_country_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_existing_actor_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	every_state = {
		limit = { random_terror_scenario_actor_state = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_valid_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	# Count only the carriers whose evolution gate this launch opens (the same
	# gates random_terror_try_spawn_actor applies).
	if = { limit = { JHX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
	if = { limit = { JIX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
	if = { limit = { JKX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage value = constant:random_terror_evolution.organized_cells compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			JNX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage value = constant:random_terror_evolution.transnational_network compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			JOX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage value = constant:random_terror_evolution.territorial_insurgency compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			JPX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage value = constant:random_terror_evolution.jihadist_international compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			JLX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_target_stage = constant:random_terror_evolution.final_jihad }
			JMX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target = global.random_terror_scenario_actor_target }
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target = global.random_terror_scenario_existing_actor_count }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target < constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	# Maximum creates the largest valid actor count the free carriers and valid
	# states support, and needs at least one actor in all.
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_actor_floor = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_global_jihad_scenario_intensity = constant:random_terror_intensity.maximum } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target value = random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target = random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target value = random_terror_scenario_valid_state_count compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target = random_terror_scenario_valid_state_count }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_actor_floor = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_actor_total = global.random_terror_scenario_existing_actor_count }
	add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_actor_total = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = world_end }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_resolved }
			check_variable = { var = random_terror_scenario_valid_country_count value = global.random_terror_scenario_country_target compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = random_terror_scenario_valid_state_count value = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = random_terror_scenario_free_carrier_count value = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = random_terror_scenario_actor_total value = random_terror_scenario_actor_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_ready = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = world_end }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_reason = constant:random_terror_scenario_reject_reason.world_end }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_valid_country_count < global.random_terror_scenario_country_target } }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_reason = constant:random_terror_scenario_reject_reason.countries }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_valid_state_count < global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target }
				check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_valid_state_count < random_terror_scenario_actor_floor }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_reason = constant:random_terror_scenario_reject_reason.states }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_reason = constant:random_terror_scenario_reject_reason.carriers }
	}
}

# Global selection of the strongest live actor for the scenario coordination
# structure.  Score: Network Authority + Territorial Control + External Supply
# plus a fixed weight for each controlled state.
random_terror_select_strongest_actor = {
	clear_global_event_target = random_terror_scenario_leader
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_strongest_score = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	every_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = num_of_controlled_states }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = constant:random_terror_tuning.scenario_leader_state_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = random_terror_network_authority }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = random_terror_territorial_control }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = random_terror_external_supply }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score > random_terror_strongest_score } }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_strongest_score = random_terror_candidate_score }
			save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_scenario_leader
		}
	}
}

# Setup Step 5 (Part 7).  From Medium upward, when at least two actors exist,
# the strongest founds the Jihadist International and the others join it.
# High and Maximum keep one rival command independent.
random_terror_scenario_form_coordination = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_actor_total = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	every_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_actor_total = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_global_jihad_scenario_intensity > constant:random_terror_intensity.low }
			check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_actor_total > constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		random_terror_select_strongest_actor = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_scenario_leader }
			event_target:random_terror_scenario_leader = {
				random_terror_create_jihadist_international_faction = yes
				set_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader
				set_country_flag = random_terror_jihadist_international_member
				set_country_flag = random_terror_later_religious_opposition_registry
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_global_jihad_scenario_intensity > constant:random_terror_intensity.medium } }
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						random_terror_is_actor = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader }
					}
					set_country_flag = random_terror_independent_command
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					random_terror_is_actor = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_independent_command }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:random_terror_scenario_leader }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
					leave_faction = yes
				}
				event_target:random_terror_scenario_leader = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
						add_to_faction = PREV
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = random_terror_jihadist_international_member
				set_country_flag = random_terror_later_religious_opposition_registry
			}
		}
		clear_global_event_target = random_terror_scenario_leader
		random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
	}
}

random_terror_scenario_seed_state = {
	random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
	set_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = global.random_terror_scenario_activity }
	set_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = global.random_terror_scenario_pressure }
	set_state_flag = random_terror_scenario_state_seeded
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_states_seeded = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.dispersed_networks } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_dispersed_cell_seeded
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.border_corridors } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_border_corridor_seeded
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_pressure_gain }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.capital_uprisings } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_capital_pressure_seeded
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_pressure_gain }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.territorial_fronts } }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_territorial_front_seeded
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_pressure_gain }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
}

random_terror_scenario_seed_country = {
	random_terror_initialize_country_state = yes
	random_terror_achievement_register_reaction_country = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_manual_scenario_disqualified
	set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_short_crisis_disqualified
	set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_long_watch_disqualified
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_city_tracking
				has_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_city_mission_succeeded
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_city_disqualified
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_scenario_target
	set_country_flag = random_terror_target_reserved
	random_terror_mark_affected_country = yes
	random_terror_schedule_recovery_check = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_response_cell_active
	set_country_flag = random_terror_government_opposition_recorded
	set_country_flag = random_terror_clerical_opposition_recorded
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_muslim_majority_reaction_registry }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_muslim_government_opposition_recorded
	}
	set_variable = { random_terror_pressure = global.random_terror_scenario_pressure }
	set_variable = { random_terror_response_legitimacy = global.random_terror_scenario_legitimacy }
	set_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = global.random_terror_scenario_response_capacity }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.capital_uprisings } }
		# Capital Uprisings open the seizure mission at once.
		capital_scope = {
			random_terror_scenario_seed_state = yes
			set_state_flag = random_terror_capital_infiltration_recorded
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_mission_subject
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_2 } }
			random_terror_begin_capital_mission = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { random_terror_state_is_eligible_incident_site = yes }
			random_terror_scenario_seed_state = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.border_corridors } }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_recorded
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_effective_type = constant:random_terror_jihad_type.territorial_fronts } }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_retake_prepared
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_civil_war_target > global.random_terror_scenario_civil_wars_started }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_civil_war_started }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_civil_war_active }
		}
		random_terror_start_pressure_civil_war = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_begun = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_civil_wars_started = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_scenario_report_sent } }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_scenario_report_sent
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.90 days = 1 }
	}
	random_terror_schedule_country_pulse = yes
}

random_terror_select_and_seed_scenario_country = {
	add_to_temp_array = { random_terror_wave_country_pool = THIS }
	clear_temp_array = random_terror_wave_country_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_scenario_candidate_country = yes }
		random_terror_add_current_country_to_wave_target_pool = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_wave_country_pool^num > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = random_terror_wave_country_pool
			random_terror_scenario_seed_country = yes
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_countries_seeded = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = random_terror_wave_country_pool
}

random_terror_scenario_seed_targets = {
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_countries_seeded = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = global.random_terror_scenario_country_target
		value = random_terror_scenario_country_index
		random_terror_select_and_seed_scenario_country = yes
	}
}

random_terror_scenario_seed_extra_states = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_extra_state_target = global.random_terror_scenario_state_target }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_extra_state_target = global.random_terror_scenario_country_target }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_scenario_extra_state_target > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = random_terror_scenario_extra_state_target
			value = random_terror_scenario_extra_state_index
			random_state = {
				limit = { random_terror_scenario_extra_state = yes }
				random_terror_scenario_seed_state = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

random_terror_scenario_seed_actors = {
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target
		value = random_terror_scenario_actor_index
		random_state = {
			limit = { random_terror_scenario_actor_state = yes }
			set_state_flag = random_terror_scenario_actor_state_reserved
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_current_state
			random_terror_try_spawn_actor = yes
		}
	}
}

random_terror_scenario_record_history = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_triggerable_scenario_history_recorded } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_scenario_previous_last_event_id = global.last_fired_event_id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_history_actor_override = THIS }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_history_actor_override_active = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		set_variable = { global.last_fired_event_id = constant:random_terror_identity.event_id }
		record_events_log_history_entry = yes
		set_variable = { global.last_fired_event_id = random_terror_scenario_previous_last_event_id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_history_actor_override_active = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_triggerable_scenario_history_recorded
	}
}

random_terror_launch_global_jihad = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	random_terror_release_dead_carriers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_can_launch_global_jihad = yes }
		random_terror_resolve_scenario_profile = yes
		random_terror_scenario_preflight = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_ready > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress
			set_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_active
			set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_setup_bypass
			random_terror_apply_scenario_evolutions = yes
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_civil_wars_started = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_actors_created = global.random_terror_scenario_existing_actor_count }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_states_seeded = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_scenario_host
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_launch_date = global.date }
			random_terror_scenario_seed_targets = yes
			random_terror_scenario_seed_extra_states = yes
			# Each created actor opens its own war with its parent during seeding.
			# Maximum enables Final Jihad, not the entity or its terminal package.
			random_terror_scenario_seed_actors = yes
			random_terror_scenario_form_coordination = yes
			random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = global.random_terror_scenario_network_reach }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = global.random_terror_scenario_unity }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = global.random_terror_scenario_readiness }
			clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_scenario_launched
			set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_launch_recorded
			# Event Logs rows for the opened evolutions; their report popups are
			# held back while setup is in progress.
			random_terror_record_scenario_evolutions = yes
			random_terror_scenario_record_history = yes
			random_terror_achievement_record_scenario_launch = yes
			clr_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_setup_bypass
			clr_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress
			clr_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_manual_request
			# The scenario's own reports: seeded governments receive the launch
			# report, every other player the rejection report.
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					exists = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_scenario_report_sent }
				}
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.6 days = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_news_sent } }
				news_event = { id = chaosx.news.310 }
				set_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_news_sent
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_terror_clear_scenario_profile = yes
			set_country_flag = random_terror_scenario_launch_blocked
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.91 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Territorial actor carriers
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

random_terror_initialize_actor = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_cleanup_complete
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_player_actor_defeat_resolved
	set_country_flag = random_terror_actor
	set_country_flag = random_terror_active_actor
	set_country_flag = random_terror_actor_package_ready
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		random_terror_achievement_initialize_country = yes
		set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_player_actor_eligible
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_network_authority } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_authority_start }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_external_supply } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_start }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_territorial_control } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_start }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_actor_setup_complete } }
		set_stability = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_stability_start
		set_war_support = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_war_support_start
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_popularities = {
			neutrality = 100
			democratic = 0
			fascism = 0
			communism = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.local } }
			set_research_slots = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_research_slots_local
		}
		else = {
			set_research_slots = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_research_slots
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = num_of_controlled_states }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_per_state }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count < constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_local } }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_local }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.regional }
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count < constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_regional }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_regional }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.transnational }
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count < constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_transnational }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_transnational }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.jihadist }
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count < constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_jihadist }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_jihadist }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.final }
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count < constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_final }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_divisions_final }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count > constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_division_cap } }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_division_count = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_division_cap }
		}
		# Technology floor, then the curated technology of the parent
		# (Part 5): a reused carrier inherits from its new parent and keeps
		# at least this floor.
		set_technology = {
			infantry_weapons = 1
			infantry_weapons1 = 1
			tech_support = 1
			tech_recon = 1
			basic_machine_tools = 1
			construction1 = 1
			radio = 1
			popup = no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = random_terror_actor_parent
				event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = {
					exists = yes
					NOT = { tag = PREV }
				}
			}
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_tech_donor }
			random_terror_inherit_actor_technology = yes
		}
		# The floor's industry branch follows the parent: dispersed only when
		# the command holds neither branch yet.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_tech = concentrated_industry }
				NOT = { has_tech = dispersed_industry }
			}
			set_technology = { dispersed_industry = 1 popup = no }
		}
		add_manpower = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_manpower_start
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_infantry_equipment_start }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_support_equipment_start }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_starting_ideas_applied } }
			add_ideas = {
				random_terror_improvised_command
				random_terror_captured_economy
				random_terror_contested_legitimacy
			}
			set_country_flag = random_terror_starting_ideas_applied
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.local } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_route_shadow_council
			create_unit = { division = "name = \"Local Security Column\" division_template = \"Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.25 start_manpower_factor = 1.00 start_equipment_factor = 0.80" owner = THIS count = random_terror_actor_division_count allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.regional } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_route_war_directorate
			create_unit = { division = "name = \"Regional Field Column\" division_template = \"Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.30 start_manpower_factor = 1.00 start_equipment_factor = 0.85" owner = THIS count = random_terror_actor_division_count allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.transnational } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_route_ideological_secretariat
			create_unit = { division = "name = \"Network Field Column\" division_template = \"Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.35 start_manpower_factor = 1.00 start_equipment_factor = 0.90" owner = THIS count = random_terror_actor_division_count allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.jihadist } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_route_jihadist_international
			set_country_flag = random_terror_jihadist_branch_active
			set_country_flag = random_terror_later_religious_opposition_registry
			create_unit = { division = "name = \"International Field Column\" division_template = \"Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.40 start_manpower_factor = 1.00 start_equipment_factor = 0.90" owner = THIS count = random_terror_actor_division_count allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.final } }
			# A profile alone does not authorize the terminal identity or bonuses.
			set_country_flag = random_terror_route_jihadist_international
			set_country_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_active
			set_country_flag = random_terror_later_religious_opposition_registry
			create_unit = { division = "name = \"International Field Column\" division_template = \"Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.40 start_manpower_factor = 1.00 start_equipment_factor = 0.90" owner = THIS count = random_terror_actor_division_count allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no }
		}
		random_terror_select_actor_leader = yes
		set_country_flag = random_terror_actor_setup_complete
	}
}

random_terror_assign_actor_cosmetic_tag = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_is_final_actor = yes }
		set_cosmetic_tag = event31_false_revelation_final
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.jihadist }
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.final }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = global.random_terror_jihadist_cosmetic_sequence }
		modulo_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index value = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.jihadist_count }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_jihadist_cosmetic_sequence = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_00 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_01 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_01 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_02 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_02 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_03 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_03 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_04 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_04 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_05 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_05 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_06 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_06 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_07 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_07 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_jihadist_route_08 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.transnational } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = global.random_terror_transnational_cosmetic_sequence }
		modulo_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index value = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.transnational_count }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_transnational_cosmetic_sequence = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_00 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_transnational_command_01 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_01 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_transnational_command_02 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_02 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_transnational_command_03 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_03 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_transnational_command_04 }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = global.random_terror_ordinary_cosmetic_sequence }
		modulo_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index value = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.ordinary_count }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_ordinary_cosmetic_sequence = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_00 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_09 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_01 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_10 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_02 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_11 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_03 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_12 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_04 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_13 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_05 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_14 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_06 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_15 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_07 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_16 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_08 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_17 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_09 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_18 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_10 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_19 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_11 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_20 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_12 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_21 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_13 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_22 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_14 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_23 }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cosmetic_cycle_index = constant:random_terror_cosmetic_cycle.index_15 } } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_ordinary_actor_24 }
	}
}

random_terror_select_leader_jlx = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_leader_slot }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = random_terror_leader_slot }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 1 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_02 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 2 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_03 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 3 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_04 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 4 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_05 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 5 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_06 ideology = despotism }

	}

		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 7 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_08 ideology = despotism }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.leader_candidate }
	random_list = {
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 0 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 0 }
			promote_character = JLX_random_terror_rasim_vey
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 1 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 1 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_02
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 2 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 2 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_03
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {

			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 3 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_jihadist_04

				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
