random_terror_select_leader_jpx = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_leader_slot }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = random_terror_leader_slot }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 1 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_02 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 2 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_03 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 3 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_04 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 4 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_05 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 5 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_06 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 6 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_01 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 7 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_02 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 8 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_03 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 9 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_04 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 10 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_05 ideology = despotism }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 11 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_06 ideology = despotism }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.leader_candidate }
	random_list = {
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 0 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 0 }
			promote_character = JPX_random_terror_arel_venn
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 1 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 1 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_02
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 2 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 2 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_03
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 3 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 3 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_04
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 4 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 4 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_05
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 5 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 5 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_revolutionary_06
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 6 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 6 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_01
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 7 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 7 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_02
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 8 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 8 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_03
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 9 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 9 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_04
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 10 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 10 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_05
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
		random_terror_draw_leader_candidate = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { random_terror_previous_leader_slot = 11 } }
			set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 11 }
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = JQX_random_terror_millenarian_06
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
			}
		}
	}
}

random_terror_select_leader_jhx = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 1 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_council_06 ideology = despotism }
		set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 0 }
		promote_character = JHX_random_terror_shadow_council
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_carrier_used_before }
		set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 1 }
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = JQX_random_terror_council_06
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
		}
	}
}

random_terror_select_leader_jix = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 1 } }
		remove_country_leader_role = { character = JQX_random_terror_council_05 ideology = despotism }
		set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 0 }
		promote_character = JIX_random_terror_war_directorate
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_carrier_used_before }
		set_variable = { random_terror_leader_slot = 1 }
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = JQX_random_terror_council_05
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: a carrier receiving a fresh actor package.
random_terror_select_actor_leader = {
	if = {
		limit = { original_tag = JHX }
		random_terror_select_leader_jhx = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { original_tag = JIX }
		random_terror_select_leader_jix = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { original_tag = JLX }
		random_terror_select_leader_jlx = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { original_tag = JNX }
		random_terror_select_leader_jnx = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { original_tag = JOX }
		random_terror_select_leader_jox = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { original_tag = JPX }
		random_terror_select_leader_jpx = yes
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_carrier_used_before
}

# COUNTRY scope: a non-terminal actor.  The refresh owns only the failure
# overlay: at most one command failure and one economy failure, chosen by the
# actor's route (Part 6 idea lifecycle table).  Collapsed Territorial Control
# or Network Authority shows Compromised Network for the Shadow Council, Rival
# Warlords for the War Directorate and Doctrinal Schism otherwise; collapsed
# External Supply shows Looting Spiral on the coerced economy, Sponsor
# Dependency under foreign sponsorship and Famine otherwise.  A failure idea
# is added only when it is absent and is then marked as owned by the overlay;
# a command failure is added only while the actor shows none, so a failure
# form chosen by a crisis focus is never doubled;
# recovery, or a route change, removes only owned ideas, so focus-granted
# spirits survive any number of actor decisions.
random_terror_refresh_actor_lifecycles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_is_actor = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control < constant:random_terror_value.low }
					check_variable = { random_terror_network_authority < constant:random_terror_value.low }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_external_supply < constant:random_terror_value.low } }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		# The route chosen in the tree comes first, then the starting profile's.
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_route_shadow_council }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.shadow_council }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_route_war_directorate }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.war_directorate }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_route_ideological_secretariat }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.ideological_secretariat }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_route_war_directorate }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.war_directorate }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_route_shadow_council }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.shadow_council }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.ideological_secretariat }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_coerced_economy }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_idea_form.looting_spiral }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_foreign_sponsorship }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_idea_form.sponsor_dependency }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.shadow_council }
				NOT = { random_terror_has_command_failure_form = yes }
			}
			add_ideas = random_terror_compromised_network
			set_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_compromised_network
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_compromised_network
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.shadow_council }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = random_terror_compromised_network
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_compromised_network
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.war_directorate }
				NOT = { random_terror_has_command_failure_form = yes }
			}
			add_ideas = random_terror_rival_warlords
			set_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_rival_warlords
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_rival_warlords
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.war_directorate }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = random_terror_rival_warlords
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_rival_warlords
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.ideological_secretariat }
				NOT = { random_terror_has_command_failure_form = yes }
			}
			add_ideas = random_terror_doctrinal_schism
			set_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_doctrinal_schism
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_doctrinal_schism
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_command_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_route = constant:random_terror_route.ideological_secretariat }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = random_terror_doctrinal_schism
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_doctrinal_schism
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_idea_form.looting_spiral }
				NOT = { has_idea = random_terror_looting_spiral }
			}
			add_ideas = random_terror_looting_spiral
			set_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_looting_spiral
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_looting_spiral
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_idea_form.looting_spiral }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = random_terror_looting_spiral
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_looting_spiral
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_idea_form.sponsor_dependency }
				NOT = { has_idea = random_terror_sponsor_dependency }
			}
			add_ideas = random_terror_sponsor_dependency
			set_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_sponsor_dependency
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_sponsor_dependency
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_idea_form.sponsor_dependency }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = random_terror_sponsor_dependency
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_sponsor_dependency
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				NOT = { has_idea = random_terror_famine }
			}
			add_ideas = random_terror_famine
			set_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_famine
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_famine
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy_failure = constant:random_terror_value.one }
						check_variable = { random_terror_lifecycle_economy = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
					}
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = random_terror_famine
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_famine
		}
	}
}

# Releases every carrier whose country no longer exists, including an actor
# that died in a peace deal without an Event 31 cleanup pass.  Callers never
# run it between a carrier claim and the state transfer that revives the tag.
random_terror_release_dead_carriers = {
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jhx_used JHX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jhx_used }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jix_used JIX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jix_used }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jkx_used JKX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jkx_used }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jlx_used JLX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jlx_used }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jmx_used JMX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jmx_used }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jnx_used JNX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jnx_used }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jox_used JOX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jox_used }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jpx_used JPX = { exists = no } } clr_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jpx_used }
}

# COUNTRY scope: a newly created actor.  Opens its war with the parent when
# the game allows it.  Ordinary spawns and Global Jihad setup share this step.
random_terror_actor_declare_parent_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_actor_parent
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { exists = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:random_terror_actor_parent }
			can_declare_war_on = event_target:random_terror_actor_parent
		}
		declare_war_on = { target = event_target:random_terror_actor_parent type = annex_everything }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the claimed carrier immediately after the spawn state was
# transferred to it.  A carrier that died without a cleanup pass still holds
# its previous lifetime, so that lifetime is cleared before the new package.
# Inputs: temp random_terror_spawn_profile, event targets
# random_terror_spawn_state and random_terror_actor_parent.
random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = random_terror_actor
				has_country_flag = random_terror_actor_setup_complete
			}
		}
		random_terror_clear_actor_runtime = yes
	}
	set_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = random_terror_spawn_profile }
	set_capital = { state = event_target:random_terror_spawn_state remember_old_capital = no }
	random_terror_initialize_actor = yes
	random_terror_assign_actor_cosmetic_tag = yes
	# The parent is marked for this actor's AI front strategy.
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_actor_parent }
		event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { set_country_flag = random_terror_parent_of_@PREV }
	}
	random_terror_actor_declare_parent_war = yes
}

# STATE scope: a Lost Local Control state.  A carrier is claimed only when it
# is free and the parent keeps its own controlled capital (Part 5).  Later
# carriers open as the crisis evolves.
random_terror_try_spawn_actor = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	random_terror_release_dead_carriers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_state_can_spawn_actor = yes }
		save_event_target_as = random_terror_spawn_state
		OWNER = {
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_spawn_former_owner
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_actor_parent
		}
		if = {
			limit = { JHX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes } }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jhx_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.local }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jhx }
			transfer_state_to = JHX
			add_core_of = JHX
			JHX = { random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				JIX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jix_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.regional }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jix }
			transfer_state_to = JIX
			add_core_of = JIX
			JIX = { random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				JKX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jkx_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.transnational }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jkx }
			transfer_state_to = JKX
			add_core_of = JKX
			JKX = { random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked
				JLX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jlx_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.jihadist }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jlx }
			transfer_state_to = JLX
			add_core_of = JLX
			JLX = { random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_5_unlocked
				JMX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jmx_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.jihadist }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jmx }
			transfer_state_to = JMX
			add_core_of = JMX
			JMX = {
				random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes
				set_country_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_active
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_1_unlocked
				JNX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jnx_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.local }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jnx }
			transfer_state_to = JNX
			add_core_of = JNX
			JNX = { random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_2_unlocked
				JOX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jox_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.regional }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jox }
			transfer_state_to = JOX
			add_core_of = JOX
			JOX = { random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_3_unlocked
				JPX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jpx_used
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_spawn_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.transnational }
			event_target:random_terror_actor_parent = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_parent_jpx }
			transfer_state_to = JPX
			add_core_of = JPX
			JPX = { random_terror_setup_spawned_carrier = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_actor_created > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
			set_state_flag = random_terror_achievement_state_lost_to_actor
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_achievement_lost_state
			event_target:random_terror_spawn_former_owner = {
				random_terror_refresh_priority_state = yes
				add_to_variable = { random_terror_states_lost_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				random_terror_achievement_record_actor_formation = yes
				random_terror_achievement_record_state_loss = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_state_count = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_start }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_territorial_actor_exists
			random_terror_emit_first_actor_news = yes
			random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress }
				add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_scenario_actors_created = constant:random_terror_value.one }
			}
		}
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Baseline civil-war, response, and actor action outcomes
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY scope: the government.  The one Event 31 civil-war starter, shared
# by the armed-cell, scenario and coup paths and by every pressure uprising.
# It never changes the government's ideology: no ruling_party is passed (the
# engine would otherwise switch the original country to it and disallow its
# elections), and the rebels always take an ideology the government does not
# hold, fascist against a democratic or communist government and democratic
# against a fascist or non-aligned one, so the engine always gets a valid
# two-sided civil war.  It does nothing while the government is already in a
# civil war of any origin.
# Input: temp random_terror_civil_war_size (revolt size fraction); zero or
# unset uses random_terror_tuning.civil_war_size; it is reset to zero on
# return so a later call in the same chain never inherits it.
# Output: temp random_terror_civil_war_begun is one only when the war really
# started, which vanilla reads the same way (has_civil_war on the new side
# right after start_civil_war, POL_scripted_effects.txt); the government then
# carries random_terror_civil_war_started.
random_terror_start_pressure_civil_war = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_begun = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_civil_war_started }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_civil_war_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = event021_civil_war_active }
			NOT = { has_civil_war = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_size > constant:random_terror_value.zero } } }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_size = constant:random_terror_tuning.civil_war_size }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_government = communism
				}
			}
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					start_civil_war = { ideology = fascism size = [RT_CW_SIZE] }
				}
				RT_CW_SIZE = "[?random_terror_civil_war_size|.2]"
			}
		}
		else = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					start_civil_war = { ideology = democratic size = [RT_CW_SIZE] }
				}
				RT_CW_SIZE = "[?random_terror_civil_war_size|.2]"
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_civil_war = yes }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_civil_war_started
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_begun = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_size = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
}

random_terror_response_reduce_target = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_national_pressure_reduction }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = -1 }
		random_terror_apply_pressure = yes
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_loss }
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
}

random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_gain }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_capacity_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_response_capacity min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_capacity.maximum }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_response_action_recorded
}

# COUNTRY scope.  Adds caller-owned temp random_terror_pending_lasting_capacity
# to the capacity that outlasts the crisis, up to random_terror_capacity.lasting_cap,
# and resets the input.
random_terror_add_lasting_capacity = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_lasting_capacity } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_lasting_capacity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_lasting_capacity = random_terror_pending_lasting_capacity }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_lasting_capacity min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_capacity.lasting_cap }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_lasting_capacity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
}

random_terror_response_action_lock = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_response_action_lock_duration = constant:random_terror_tuning.decision_action_cooldown_days }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_action_recent days = [LOCK_DAYS] } }
		LOCK_DAYS = "[?random_terror_response_action_lock_duration|.0]"
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_response_action_recorded
}

random_terror_decision_establish_response_cell = {
	random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = yes
	# The joint cell is the coordination capacity of Part 3.
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_capacity.response_cell_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_response_capacity min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_capacity.maximum }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_response_cell_active
	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
}

random_terror_decision_protect_transport = {
	random_terror_response_reduce_target = yes
	random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_transport_protection_active
	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
}

# Relief calms copycat pressure everywhere it lands.  In a recovering state it
# also starves the residual cells of local support (matrix: neglect preserves
# dormant activity), which is how Restore Civil Authority advances.
random_terror_decision_support_victims = {
	random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = yes
	add_stability = constant:random_terror_tuning.government_stability_support
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			random_terror_achievement_record_victim_support = yes
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
			# Relief First (first emphasis) also starves active cells of support.
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery
						ROOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_emphasis_victims }
					}
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_loss }
			}
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
}

random_terror_decision_intelligence_sweep = {
	random_terror_response_reduce_target = yes
	# Response Capacity turns the sweep into a deeper strike on the cell.
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = random_terror_response_capacity }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_pending_activity_delta min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_capacity.effective_cap }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_capacity.sweep_activity_per_point }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = { random_terror_add_state_activity = yes }
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.global_network_intelligence_reduction }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_intelligence_breakthrough
	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
}

random_terror_decision_security_surge = {
	random_terror_response_reduce_target = yes
	# Rapid Crackdown (first emphasis): the surge hits the cell harder.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = random_terror_emphasis_coercion
			has_event_target = random_terror_action_state
		}
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_loss }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
			random_terror_add_state_activity = yes
		}
	}
	# A surge without an intelligence breakthrough scatters the cell instead
	# of catching it, unless a defection channel or allied intelligence
	# keeps track of the survivors.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_action_state
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_intelligence_breakthrough }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_defection_channel_open }
		}
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_joint_intelligence_received }
					check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_value.zero }
				}
				random_terror_mark_survivors_displaced = yes
			}
		}
	}
	random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_security_surge_active
	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope: the government acting on its own hostage or uprising
# subject, saved as random_terror_action_state.  A surge storms the hostage
# site: against an armed cell only part of the captives get out.  Against an
# uprising it breaks the rising outright.
random_terror_emergency_surge_outcome = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject_@ROOT }
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency }
					set_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_partly_freed_@ROOT
				}
				else = {
					set_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_recovered_@ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@ROOT }
				set_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_stopped_@ROOT
			}
		}
	}
}

	random_terror_response_reduce_target = yes

	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
}

# The decision itself sets the timed random_terror_movement_restrictions_active
# flag.  Every use after the first draws public condemnation, and each use
# raises the price of the next one.
random_terror_decision_movement_restrictions = {
	random_terror_response_reduce_target = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_movement_stability_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_stability_restriction_delta }
	multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_movement_stability_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
	add_stability = random_terror_movement_stability_delta
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_movement_restrictions_uses > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
		random_terror_add_public_government_abuse_condemnation = yes
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_movement_restrictions_uses = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	random_terror_update_movement_restrictions_cost = yes
	random_terror_achievement_record_movement_restrictions = yes
	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope: a government.  Sets the current Stability and support
# equipment price of Temporary Movement Restrictions from its earlier uses
# (random_terror_movement_restrictions_uses): each use adds one repeat step,
# up to movement_repeat_max_steps.
random_terror_update_movement_restrictions_cost = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_movement_repeat_steps = random_terror_movement_restrictions_uses }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_movement_repeat_steps min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_cost.movement_repeat_max_steps }
	set_variable = { random_terror_movement_stability_cost = random_terror_movement_repeat_steps }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_movement_stability_cost = constant:random_terror_cost.movement_stability_repeat_step }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_movement_stability_cost = constant:random_terror_cost.movement_stability }
	set_variable = { random_terror_movement_equipment_cost = random_terror_movement_repeat_steps }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_movement_equipment_cost = constant:random_terror_cost.movement_support_equipment_repeat_step }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_movement_equipment_cost = constant:random_terror_cost.movement_support_equipment }
}

random_terror_decision_security_reform = {
	random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_reform_capacity_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_response_capacity min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_capacity.maximum }
	# Part of the reform outlasts this crisis.
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_lasting_capacity = constant:random_terror_capacity.reform_lasting_gain }
	random_terror_add_lasting_capacity = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_security_reform_complete
	random_terror_response_action_lock = yes
	hidden_effect = { random_terror_close_country_recovery = yes }
}

# Actor decisions.  Every route spends authority or supply only after the
# decision layer has checked the concrete resource cost.  No action derives
# eligibility from religion, ethnicity, nationality, refugee status, or normal
# ideology.  The fictional religious branch records opposition explicitly
# when it is entered, so its presence never becomes a baseline target weight.

# COUNTRY scope: an actor, ROOT of the calling decision or focus.  Raises one
# formation of the carrier's shared "Infantry Division" template.  Inputs
# (temp): random_terror_unit_experience, random_terror_unit_equipment,
# random_terror_unit_manpower and random_terror_unit_id.  With a saved
# random_terror_action_state the formation musters there; otherwise it forms
# in the actor's own provinces.  Callers keep the actor under
# actor_division_cap.
random_terror_actor_raise_formation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "division_template = \"Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = [RT_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [RT_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [RT_MANPOWER]"
						owner = ROOT
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
						id = [RT_UNIT_ID]
					}
				}
			}
			RT_EXPERIENCE = "[?random_terror_unit_experience|.2]"
			RT_EQUIPMENT = "[?random_terror_unit_equipment|.2]"
			RT_MANPOWER = "[?random_terror_unit_manpower|.2]"
			RT_UNIT_ID = "[?random_terror_unit_id|.0]"
		}
	}
	else = {
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "division_template = \"Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = [RT_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [RT_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [RT_MANPOWER]"
					owner = ROOT
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
					id = [RT_UNIT_ID]
				}
			}
			RT_EXPERIENCE = "[?random_terror_unit_experience|.2]"
			RT_EQUIPMENT = "[?random_terror_unit_equipment|.2]"
			RT_MANPOWER = "[?random_terror_unit_manpower|.2]"
			RT_UNIT_ID = "[?random_terror_unit_id|.0]"
		}
	}
}

# Sets random_terror_unit_id to base + the action state's id, so each state's
# militia can later be converted without touching any other formation.
# Input: temp random_terror_unit_id holding the base.
random_terror_actor_key_unit_to_action_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_state_key = THIS }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = random_terror_unit_state_key }
	}
}

# A focus reward that reuses an actor helper sets the temp variable
# random_terror_focus_context first, so completing a focus never starts the
# decision cooldown.
random_terror_actor_action_common = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { random_terror_focus_context = constant:random_terror_value.one } } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_actor_action_lock_duration = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_action_cooldown_days }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_actor_action_lock days = [LOCK_DAYS] } }
			LOCK_DAYS = "[?random_terror_actor_action_lock_duration|.0]"
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.readiness_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
	random_terror_refresh_actor_lifecycles = yes
}

# One under-strength local militia.  In a targeted state it is keyed to that
# state and marks it for later conversion.
random_terror_actor_raise_local_militia = {
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_militia_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_experience = constant:random_terror_unit.militia_experience }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_equipment = constant:random_terror_unit.militia_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_manpower = constant:random_terror_unit.militia_manpower }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = constant:random_terror_unit.militia_id_base }
	random_terror_actor_key_unit_to_action_state = yes
	# Only a militia that actually forms marks its state for conversion.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_action_state
			num_divisions < constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_division_cap
		}
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = { set_state_flag = random_terror_actor_militia_present }
	}
	random_terror_focus_raise_formation = yes
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

# Defecting personnel arrive as one seasoned formation.
random_terror_actor_integrate_defectors = {
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_defector_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_military_defection_recorded
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_experience = constant:random_terror_unit.defector_experience }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_equipment = constant:random_terror_unit.defector_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_manpower = constant:random_terror_unit.defector_manpower }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = constant:random_terror_unit.defector_id }
	random_terror_focus_raise_formation = yes
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

# Restores the damaged depot as a working arms factory, or as road and rail
# capacity when the state has no free factory slot.
random_terror_actor_repair_captured_depot = {
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_depot_gain }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					has_state_flag = random_terror_depot_compromised
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_depot_compromised }
				save_event_target_as = random_terror_action_state
				clr_state_flag = random_terror_depot_compromised
			}
		}
		else = {
			capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_action_state }
		}
	}
	event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
		if = {
			limit = { free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 } }
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		else = {
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
	}
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

random_terror_actor_open_external_corridor = {
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_corridor_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_active
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

random_terror_actor_seek_sponsor = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_sponsor_contact_recorded
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

random_terror_actor_support_foreign_cell = {
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_foreign_cell_supported
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

# Moves one threatened cell out of a state the government has swept.  The
# next government raid there finds empty safe houses and can at best be a
# partial success.  The move costs the command some local hold unless the
# Continuity Protocol focus is complete.
random_terror_actor_relocate_network = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state } }
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes
				has_state_flag = random_terror_intelligence_sweep_completed
				NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_cell_relocated }
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_action_state
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = { set_state_flag = random_terror_cell_relocated }
	}
	# A command under the Continuity Protocol moves along prepared routes.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_continuity_protocol } }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_minor_gain }
	}
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

# Retrains the militia of the targeted state into one regular formation: the
# militia raised there (keyed by state) is retired and replaced, so no unit is
# duplicated.  Without a target state, as a focus reward, it trains one new
# regular formation.
random_terror_actor_convert_militia = {
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_convert_gain }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = constant:random_terror_unit.militia_id_base }
		random_terror_actor_key_unit_to_action_state = yes
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				delete_unit = {
					id = [RT_UNIT_ID]
					disband = no
				}
			}
			RT_UNIT_ID = "[?random_terror_unit_id|.0]"
		}
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = { clr_state_flag = random_terror_actor_militia_present }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_experience = constant:random_terror_unit.converted_experience }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_equipment = constant:random_terror_unit.converted_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_manpower = constant:random_terror_unit.converted_manpower }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = constant:random_terror_unit.converted_id_base }
	random_terror_actor_key_unit_to_action_state = yes
	random_terror_focus_raise_formation = yes
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

# The command's declared program mobilizes its supporters.  One disciplined
# formation musters (in random_terror_action_state when the decision saved
# one), a second one after Codify the Doctrine, and the command gains
# Mobilized Cadres for mobilization_days.  The timed active flag keeps the
# decision closed until the mobilization ends; the recorded flag keeps the
# Jihadist International entry open afterwards.
random_terror_actor_ideological_mobilization = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_experience = constant:random_terror_unit.cadre_experience }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_equipment = constant:random_terror_unit.cadre_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_manpower = constant:random_terror_unit.cadre_manpower }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = constant:random_terror_unit.cadre_id }
	random_terror_focus_raise_formation = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_doctrine_codified }
		random_terror_focus_raise_formation = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mobilization_days = constant:random_terror_mobilization.mobilization_days }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_mobilized_cadres days = random_terror_mobilization_days }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_ideological_mobilization_active days = [MOBILIZATION_DAYS] } }
		MOBILIZATION_DAYS = "[?random_terror_mobilization_days|.0]"
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_ideological_mobilization_recorded
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

# Offices replace improvised rule in a held state: roads and rail are rebuilt
# and the command's hold on its territory and authority firms up.
random_terror_actor_build_civil_administration = {
	random_terror_grant_advanced_research_slot = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_convert_gain }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_authority_civil_admin_gain }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state } }
		capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_action_state }
	}
	event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
		add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
	}
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

	add_to_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_extraction_gain }
	random_terror_add_public_actor_atrocity_condemnation = yes
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes

# Volunteers arrive through the network: a net manpower intake and one
# experienced formation at the actor's own provinces.
random_terror_actor_recruit_foreign_fighters = {
	add_manpower = constant:random_terror_tuning.foreign_fighter_manpower
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:random_terror_tuning.foreign_fighter_equipment }
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_action_network_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_foreign_fighters_cooldown = constant:random_terror_tuning.foreign_fighters_cooldown_days }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_foreign_fighters_recruited days = [COOLDOWN_DAYS] } }
		COOLDOWN_DAYS = "[?random_terror_foreign_fighters_cooldown|.0]"
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_experience = constant:random_terror_unit.foreign_experience }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_equipment = constant:random_terror_unit.foreign_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_manpower = constant:random_terror_unit.foreign_manpower }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = constant:random_terror_unit.foreign_id }
	random_terror_focus_raise_formation = yes
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

# Creates one Event 31 faction through the current faction-template consumer.
# Each wrapper owns its emblem and restores faction-creation permission when it
# had to grant that permission for the transaction.
random_terror_create_regional_coordination_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_faction = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_rule = can_create_factions } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule
			set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
		}
		create_faction_from_template = {
			template = faction_template_generic
			name = random_terror_regional_coordination_faction
			icon = GFX_faction_logo_random_terror_regional_coordination
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule }
			clear_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule
		}
	}
}

random_terror_create_network_confederation_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_faction = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_rule = can_create_factions } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule
			set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
		}
		create_faction_from_template = {
			template = faction_template_generic
			name = random_terror_network_confederation_faction
			icon = GFX_faction_logo_random_terror_network_confederation
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule }
			clear_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule
		}
	}
}
