random_terror_create_final_jihad_command_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}
		else = { leave_faction = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_faction = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_rule = can_create_factions } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule
			set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
		}
		create_faction_from_template = {
			template = faction_template_generic_dominance
			name = random_terror_final_command_faction
			icon = GFX_faction_logo_random_terror_final_jihad_command
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule }
			clear_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_temporary_faction_creation_rule
		}
	}
}

random_terror_actor_join_jihadist_international = {
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
}

random_terror_actor_accelerate_revelation = {
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_can_begin_false_revelation = yes }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_requested
		random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_pending
				NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_accelerated }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_false_revelation_days }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days min = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.minimum max = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.accelerated_maximum }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days }
			set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
			chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_due_date = global.date }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_due_date = chaosx_date_offset }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_accelerated
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.94 days = random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days }
		}
		else = {
			random_terror_schedule_false_revelation = yes
		}
	}
}

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Focus rewards.  One effect per actor focus whose reward is a map, unit,
# faction or decision change.  The focus shows a plain custom tooltip and runs
# the effect hidden.  COUNTRY scope: the actor completing the focus (ROOT).
# Amounts live in the random_terror_focus_reward and random_terror_unit
# constants.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# STATE scope: an Event 31 crisis state an actor focus presses.  Input: temp
# random_terror_focus_activity_push.  Activity and pressure rise and the stage,
# state modifier and priority state follow.
random_terror_focus_push_state_activity = {
	random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = random_terror_focus_activity_push }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_pressure_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
}

# Continuity Protocol: fallback headquarters in the key towns of the command's
# seat.  While random_terror_focus_continuity_protocol is set, Relocate the
# Network no longer costs Territorial Control.
random_terror_focus_reward_continuity_protocol = {
	capital_scope = {
		add_building_construction = {
			type = bunker
			level = 1
			province = { all_provinces = yes limit_to_victory_point = yes }
			instant_build = yes
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_continuity_protocol
}

# Establish Political Offices: the seat becomes a core state, and the
# random_terror_focus_political_offices flag opens Build a Civil
# Administration for the Ideological Secretariat.
random_terror_focus_reward_political_offices = {
	capital_scope = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT } }
			add_core_of = ROOT
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_authority_civil_admin_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_political_offices
	random_terror_grant_advanced_research_slot = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope: an actor at its first setup.  Input: event target
# random_terror_tech_donor, the government it rose from (or, for the final
# command formed by annexation, the command it absorbs).  Copies a curated
# set of the donor's technology (Part 5 Starting research and technology):
# infantry and support weapons, support companies, artillery, motorization
# basics, industry and construction including the donor's industry branch,
# and electronics.  It never copies doctrines, naval, air, nuclear, rocket or
# special-project technology, and never removes a technology, so a reused
# carrier keeps at least its floor.
random_terror_inherit_actor_technology = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_tech_donor
			event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { exists = yes }
		}
		# Infantry and support weapons
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = infantry_weapons2 } } set_technology = { infantry_weapons2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = improved_infantry_weapons } } set_technology = { improved_infantry_weapons = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = improved_infantry_weapons_2 } } set_technology = { improved_infantry_weapons_2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = advanced_infantry_weapons } } set_technology = { advanced_infantry_weapons = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = advanced_infantry_weapons2 } } set_technology = { advanced_infantry_weapons2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = infantry_at } } set_technology = { infantry_at = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = infantry_at2 } } set_technology = { infantry_at2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = support_weapons } } set_technology = { support_weapons = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = support_weapons2 } } set_technology = { support_weapons2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = support_weapons3 } } set_technology = { support_weapons3 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = support_weapons4 } } set_technology = { support_weapons4 = 1 popup = no } }
		# Support companies
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_engineers } } set_technology = { tech_engineers = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_engineers2 } } set_technology = { tech_engineers2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_recon2 } } set_technology = { tech_recon2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_military_police } } set_technology = { tech_military_police = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_field_hospital } } set_technology = { tech_field_hospital = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_logistics_company } } set_technology = { tech_logistics_company = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_signal_company } } set_technology = { tech_signal_company = 1 popup = no } }
		# Artillery
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = gw_artillery } } set_technology = { gw_artillery = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = interwar_artillery } } set_technology = { interwar_artillery = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = artillery1 } } set_technology = { artillery1 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = artillery2 } } set_technology = { artillery2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = artillery3 } } set_technology = { artillery3 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = interwar_antitank } } set_technology = { interwar_antitank = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = antitank1 } } set_technology = { antitank1 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = antitank2 } } set_technology = { antitank2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = interwar_antiair } } set_technology = { interwar_antiair = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = antiair1 } } set_technology = { antiair1 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = antiair2 } } set_technology = { antiair2 = 1 popup = no } }
		# Motorization basics
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = tech_trucks } } set_technology = { tech_trucks = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = motorised_infantry } } set_technology = { motorised_infantry = 1 popup = no } }
		# Industry and construction
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = improved_machine_tools } } set_technology = { improved_machine_tools = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = advanced_machine_tools } } set_technology = { advanced_machine_tools = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = construction2 } } set_technology = { construction2 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = construction3 } } set_technology = { construction3 = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = construction4 } } set_technology = { construction4 = 1 popup = no } }
		# Electronics
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = electronic_mechanical_engineering } } set_technology = { electronic_mechanical_engineering = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = improved_radio } } set_technology = { improved_radio = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = mechanical_computing } } set_technology = { mechanical_computing = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = computing_machine } } set_technology = { computing_machine = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = basic_encryption } } set_technology = { basic_encryption = 1 popup = no } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = basic_decryption } } set_technology = { basic_decryption = 1 popup = no } }
		# The donor's industry branch, never both branches.
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = concentrated_industry }
				NOT = { has_tech = dispersed_industry }
			}
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = concentrated_industry } } set_technology = { concentrated_industry = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = concentrated_industry2 } } set_technology = { concentrated_industry2 = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = concentrated_industry3 } } set_technology = { concentrated_industry3 = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = concentrated_industry4 } } set_technology = { concentrated_industry4 = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = concentrated_industry5 } } set_technology = { concentrated_industry5 = 1 popup = no } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = dispersed_industry }
				NOT = { has_tech = concentrated_industry }
			}
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = dispersed_industry } } set_technology = { dispersed_industry = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = dispersed_industry2 } } set_technology = { dispersed_industry2 = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = dispersed_industry3 } } set_technology = { dispersed_industry3 = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = dispersed_industry4 } } set_technology = { dispersed_industry4 = 1 popup = no } }
			if = { limit = { event_target:random_terror_tech_donor = { has_tech = dispersed_industry5 } } set_technology = { dispersed_industry5 = 1 popup = no } }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: an actor.  A transnational or jihadist command gains its
# fourth research slot once, from Build a Civil Administration (decision, or
# Codify the Doctrine) or Establish Political Offices; their tooltips show it
# only when random_terror_can_gain_advanced_research_slot holds.  Local
# commands stay at actor_research_slots_local, the standard command at
# actor_research_slots and the final command at actor_research_slots_final;
# runtime cleanup clears the record.
random_terror_grant_advanced_research_slot = {
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_can_gain_advanced_research_slot = yes }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_research_slots = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_research_slots_advanced
			set_country_flag = random_terror_advanced_research_slot
		}
	}
}

# Trace the Border Corridors: claims on every crisis state that borders the
# command's territory and that it does not own.
random_terror_focus_reward_trace_corridors = {
	every_controlled_state = {
		every_neighbor_state = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes
			}
			add_claim_by = ROOT
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_border_corridors_traced
}

# Open the Next Front: one regular formation musters in an owned border state
# facing a claimed state the command does not hold, and the cells in that
# claimed state are pressed harder.  At the division limit the command takes
# the formation's rifles into its stockpile instead.
random_terror_focus_reward_open_next_front = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				any_neighbor_state = {
					is_claimed_by = ROOT
					NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				any_neighbor_state = {
					is_claimed_by = ROOT
					NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_action_state
			random_neighbor_state = {
				limit = {
					is_claimed_by = ROOT
					NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
					random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_focus_activity_push = constant:random_terror_focus_reward.next_front_activity }
				random_terror_focus_push_state_activity = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_divisions < constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_division_cap }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_experience = constant:random_terror_unit.front_experience }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_equipment = constant:random_terror_unit.front_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_manpower = constant:random_terror_unit.front_manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_unit_id = constant:random_terror_unit.front_id }
		random_terror_actor_raise_formation = yes
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:random_terror_cost.actor_defector_equipment }
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_militia_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_next_front_open
}

# Break the Last Corridor: every claimed state the command holds, other than a
# national capital, passes into its ownership.
random_terror_focus_reward_break_last_corridor = {
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
			is_claimed_by = ROOT
			NOT = { is_capital = yes }
		}
		transfer_state_to = ROOT
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_convert_gain }
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_last_corridor_broken
}

# Forge the Seal: every state the command owns and holds becomes a core state.
random_terror_focus_reward_forge_seal = {
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = {
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
		}
		add_core_of = ROOT
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_reach_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_fictional_seal
}

# Lead the Coordination Structure: a command that is free or already leads a
# faction founds or keeps the Jihadist International, takes the network
# leadership when nobody holds it, and brings in every AI-run International
# actor that is free, at peace with it and not an independent command.  A command
# serving in another faction instead receives courier support.
random_terror_focus_reward_lead_coordination = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_in_faction = yes }
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_faction = yes } }
			random_terror_create_jihadist_international_faction = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_country = {
						NOT = { tag = ROOT }
						random_terror_is_actor = yes
						has_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader
		}
		set_country_flag = random_terror_jihadist_international_member
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { tag = ROOT }
				random_terror_is_actor = yes
				OR = {
					random_terror_is_jihadist_actor = yes
					has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_route_jihadist_international
				}
				NOT = { is_in_faction = yes }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_independent_command }
				NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				# A command run by a player joins only through its own choice.
				is_ai = yes
			}
			ROOT = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
					add_to_faction = PREV
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = random_terror_jihadist_international_member
			set_country_flag = random_terror_later_religious_opposition_registry
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = constant:random_terror_cost.actor_depot_support_equipment }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = constant:random_terror_cost.actor_corridor_convoys }
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_authority_civil_admin_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_coordination_structure
	random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
}

# Challenge Rival Governments: every crisis state of another government that
# borders the command's territory is pressed harder, and the command gains a
# war goal to topple one government it is not yet fighting, preferring the
# owner of a state in Armed Insurgency or Lost Local Control.
random_terror_focus_reward_challenge_rivals = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_focus_activity_push = constant:random_terror_focus_reward.rival_pressure_activity }
	every_controlled_state = {
		every_neighbor_state = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes
				OWNER = { NOT = { random_terror_is_actor = yes } }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied }
			}
			set_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
			random_terror_focus_push_state_activity = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				any_neighbor_state = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency
						has_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
					}
					OWNER = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
			}
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				any_neighbor_state = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency
						has_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
					}
					OWNER = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
			}
			random_neighbor_state = {
				limit = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = random_terror_state_armed_insurgency
						has_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
					}
					OWNER = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
				OWNER = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_rival_government }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				any_neighbor_state = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
					OWNER = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
			}
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				any_neighbor_state = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
					OWNER = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
			}
			random_neighbor_state = {
				limit = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
					OWNER = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
				OWNER = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_rival_government }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_rival_government }
		create_wargoal = {
			type = topple_government
			target = event_target:random_terror_rival_government
		}
	}
	every_controlled_state = {
		every_neighbor_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied }
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_rival_pressure_applied
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_rival_governments_challenged
	set_country_flag = random_terror_muslim_government_opposition_recorded
}

# Synchronize the Columns: the command and every Event 31 actor fighting in
# its faction open a timed Synchronized Offensive together.
random_terror_focus_reward_synchronize_columns = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_focus_modifier_days = constant:random_terror_focus_reward.synchronized_offensive_days }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_synchronized_offensive days = random_terror_focus_modifier_days }
	every_faction_member = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { tag = ROOT }
			random_terror_is_actor = yes
			has_war = yes
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_synchronized_offensive days = random_terror_focus_modifier_days }
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_convert_gain }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_corridor_gain }
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_columns_synchronized
}

# Secure the Capital Approach: claims on the capital state of every neighbour
# at war with the command, and better roads in one owned border state facing
# an enemy.
random_terror_focus_reward_secure_capital_approach = {
	every_enemy_country = {
		limit = { is_neighbor_of = ROOT }
		capital_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_claimed_by = ROOT }
				}
				add_claim_by = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				any_neighbor_state = { CONTROLLER = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { any_neighbor_state = { CONTROLLER = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_convert_gain }
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_corridor_gain }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_capital_approach_secured
}

# Fracture the Sky: every neighbour at war with the command is shaken by the
# rupture for a time.
random_terror_focus_reward_fracture_the_sky = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_focus_modifier_days = constant:random_terror_focus_reward.sky_fracture_days }
	every_enemy_country = {
		limit = { is_neighbor_of = ROOT }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_sky_fracture_shock days = random_terror_focus_modifier_days }
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.readiness_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_focus_sky_fractured
}

random_terror_clamp_actor_values = {
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_network_authority min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_authority_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_external_supply min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_territorial_control min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_cap }
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Event-system dispatch, actor state transitions, and terminal contract
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

random_terror_prepare_dispatch = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	random_terror_reset_repeatable_cycle = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_prefire_ready = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_can_prepare_dispatch = yes }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_global_lock
		set_global_flag = random_terror_dispatch_armed
		set_global_flag = random_terror_automatic_wave_pending
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_automatic_wave_history_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_automatic_wave_id = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_dispatch_host
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_prefire_ready = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
}

random_terror_dispatch_cleanup = {
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_dispatch_armed
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_global_lock
	clear_global_event_target = random_terror_dispatch_host
}

random_terror_scenario_cleanup = {
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_setup_in_progress
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_setup_bypass
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_manual_request
	every_country = {
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_scenario_target
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_target_reserved
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_scenario_report_sent
	}
	every_state = {
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_scenario_state_seeded
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_scenario_actor_state_reserved
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_dispersed_cell_seeded
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_border_corridor_seeded
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_capital_pressure_seeded
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_territorial_front_seeded
	}
	clear_global_event_target = random_terror_scenario_host
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_ready
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_preflight_reason
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_existing_actor_count
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_new_actor_target
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_actors_created
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_countries_seeded
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_states_seeded
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_scenario_civil_wars_started
}

random_terror_refresh_global_values = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	random_terror_release_dead_carriers = yes
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_crisis_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_active_country_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_viable_actor_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_sponsor_support_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_failed_response_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_successful_response_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_restored_country_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_leadership_rivalry_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_variable = { global.random_terror_stable_leadership_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	every_country = {
		if = {
			limit = { random_terror_country_has_active_state = yes }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_active_country_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_viable_actor_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_state_count = num_of_controlled_states }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_control = random_terror_territorial_control }
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = random_terror_network_authority }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_sponsor_contact_recorded }
				add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_sponsor_support_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_idea = random_terror_compromised_network
						has_idea = random_terror_rival_warlords
						has_idea = random_terror_doctrinal_schism
						has_country_flag = random_terror_network_leadership_contested
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_leadership_rivalry_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = random_terror_network_authority value = constant:random_terror_value.unity_gate compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					NOT = { has_idea = random_terror_compromised_network }
					NOT = { has_idea = random_terror_rival_warlords }
					NOT = { has_idea = random_terror_doctrinal_schism }
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_stable_leadership_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = random_terror_failed_raid_memory
					has_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_failure
					has_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_failure
					has_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_failure
					has_country_flag = random_terror_coup_successful
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_failed_response_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_success
					has_country_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_success
					has_country_flag = random_terror_services_restored
					has_country_flag = random_terror_security_reform_complete
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_successful_response_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_success
					has_country_flag = random_terror_security_reform_complete
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_restored_country_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
	every_state = {
		limit = { random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_crisis_state_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_territorial_control min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
}

# Any scope.  Event 31's source in the shared world-threat aggregate (Part 10
# World-threat aggregate), following the holy realm pattern: the owner sets
# or clears world_threat_source_random_terror from the world state
# (random_terror_world_threat_qualifies), then rebuilds the aggregate.
# Ordinary cells never set it.  It runs only at transaction points: the
# global-value refresh (waves, takeovers, retakes, sponsorship, absorption,
# leadership contests, revelation scheduling and cycle reset), Final Jihad
# activation, Jihadist International founding, actor formation and cleanup,
# the terminal commit, the revelation and defeat; no periodic hook calls it.
random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = {
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_world_threat_qualifies = yes }
		set_global_flag = world_threat_source_random_terror
	}
	else = {
		clr_global_flag = world_threat_source_random_terror
	}
	refresh_world_threat_state = yes
}

random_terror_try_takeover_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_is_actor = yes
			check_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control > constant:random_terror_value.territorial_gate }
			has_event_target = random_terror_action_state
		}
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			CONTROLLER = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_takeover_previous_controller }
			if = {
				limit = {
					state_population_k > constant:random_terror_value.zero
					NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control }
					NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
				}
				set_state_flag = random_terror_achievement_state_lost_to_actor
				save_event_target_as = random_terror_achievement_lost_state
				transfer_state_to = ROOT
				add_core_of = ROOT
				set_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
				set_state_flag = random_terror_territorial_takeover_recorded
				if = {
					limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_takeover_previous_controller }
					event_target:random_terror_takeover_previous_controller = {
						random_terror_refresh_priority_state = yes
						add_to_variable = { random_terror_states_lost_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
						random_terror_achievement_record_actor_formation = yes
						random_terror_achievement_record_state_loss = yes
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_event_target = random_terror_takeover_previous_controller
						event_target:random_terror_takeover_previous_controller = { num_of_controlled_states > constant:random_terror_value.zero }
					}
					set_state_flag = random_terror_territorial_partition_recorded
					event_target:random_terror_takeover_previous_controller = { set_country_flag = random_terror_territorial_partition_recorded }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_event_target = random_terror_takeover_previous_controller
						event_target:random_terror_takeover_previous_controller = { num_of_controlled_states < constant:random_terror_value.one }
					}
					event_target:random_terror_takeover_previous_controller = {
						set_country_flag = random_terror_government_takeover_recorded
						set_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_war_without_capital_disqualified
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_start }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_strategic_victories = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_state_takeover_recorded
		random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
		random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
		random_terror_schedule_false_revelation = yes
	}
}

random_terror_start_pressure_uprising = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			state_population_k > constant:random_terror_value.zero
			check_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.minimum_pressure_for_uprising }
			random_terror_state_uprising_window_open = yes
		}
		# A rising is under way only while its controller holds the Stop the
		# Coordinated Uprising mission for this state, which clears the flag
		# when it ends; a rising without that mission leaves only the date
		# window below, so the state can never be locked for good.
		CONTROLLER = {
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@PREV } }
				PREV = { set_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_recent }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_cooldown_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		set_variable = { random_terror_uprising_cooldown_until = global.date }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_uprising_cooldown_until = chaosx_date_offset }
		# The shared starter keeps the government's ideology, picks rebels of
		# another ideology and records the war only when it really began.
		CONTROLLER = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_civil_war_started }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = random_civil_war_active }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = event021_civil_war_active }
					NOT = { has_civil_war = yes }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_size = constant:random_terror_tuning.civil_war_size }
				if = {
					limit = { event_target:random_terror_uprising_state = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.severe } } }
					set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_size = constant:random_terror_tuning.civil_war_size_severe }
				}
				random_terror_start_pressure_civil_war = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_begun = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
					set_country_flag = random_terror_pressure_uprising_recorded
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

random_terror_reconcile_pressure_uprising = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.minimum_pressure_for_uprising }
					random_terror_state_uprising_window_open = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = random_terror_uprising_state
				random_terror_start_pressure_uprising = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Decision transaction and mission outcome helpers
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# These helpers are deliberately state-targeted.  A decision pays its concrete
# cost before entering one of these effects; the effect records the commitment,
# changes the pressure/network ledger, and leaves any map transfer or foreign
# assistance to an explicit mission/consent path.

random_terror_resolve_targeted_raid_outcome = {
	random_terror_initialize_country_state = yes
	# Response Capacity adds to the clean-success weight of every band.
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_capacity_clean_bonus = random_terror_response_capacity }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_capacity_clean_bonus min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_capacity.effective_cap }
	multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_capacity_clean_bonus = constant:random_terror_capacity.raid_clean_weight_per_point }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.medium }
			if = {
				limit = {
					CONTROLLER = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_response_legitimacy > constant:random_terror_value.high }
					}
					check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity < constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_entrenched_threshold }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.high }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_state_activity > constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_armed_threshold }
						CONTROLLER = {
							check_variable = { random_terror_response_legitimacy < constant:random_terror_value.low }
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.low }
			}
			# Intelligence Priority (first emphasis): the first raid of the period
			# draws on better information, one band up.
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_emphasis_first_raid } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.low } }
					set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.medium }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.high }
				}
				ROOT = { clr_country_flag = random_terror_emphasis_first_raid }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.high } }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_clean = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_clean }
				add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_clean = random_terror_capacity_clean_bonus }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_costly = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_costly }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_partial = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_partial }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_failure = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_failure }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_abusive = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_abusive }
				random_list = {
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_clean = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_costly = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.costly_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_partial = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_failure = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_abusive = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.abusive_failure } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_raid_band = constant:random_terror_intensity.low } }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_clean = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_low_clean }
				add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_clean = random_terror_capacity_clean_bonus }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_costly = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_low_costly }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_partial = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_low_partial }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_failure = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_low_failure }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_abusive = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_low_abusive }
				random_list = {
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_clean = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_costly = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.costly_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_partial = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_failure = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_low_abusive = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.abusive_failure } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_clean = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_mid_clean }
				add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_clean = random_terror_capacity_clean_bonus }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_costly = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_mid_costly }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_partial = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_mid_partial }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_failure = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_mid_failure }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_abusive = constant:random_terror_runtime_weight.raid_mid_abusive }
				random_list = {
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_clean = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_costly = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.costly_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_partial = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_failure = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure } }
					random_terror_draw_runtime_raid_mid_abusive = { set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.abusive_failure } }
				}
			}
			# A relocated cell leaves only empty safe houses for the next raid.
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_cell_relocated
					OR = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
						check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.costly_success }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success }
			}
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_cell_relocated
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success } }
				subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.clean_operation_activity_reduction }
				subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
				set_state_flag = random_terror_raid_clean_success
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject_@ROOT }
					set_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_recovered_@ROOT
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@ROOT }
					set_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_stopped_@ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.costly_success } }
				subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.costly_operation_activity_reduction }
				subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
				set_state_flag = random_terror_raid_costly_success
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject_@ROOT }
					set_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_recovered_@ROOT
					set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_tuning.civilian_deaths_minor }
					random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success } }
				subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.partial_operation_activity_reduction }
				subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_loss }
				set_state_flag = random_terror_raid_partial_success
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject_@ROOT }
					set_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_partly_freed_@ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure }
						check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.abusive_failure }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.failed_operation_pressure_gain }
				add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
				set_state_flag = random_terror_failed_raid_recorded
				CONTROLLER = { set_country_flag = random_terror_failed_raid_memory }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.abusive_failure } }
				add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.abusive_operation_pressure_gain }
				set_state_flag = random_terror_raid_abusive_failure
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_tuning.civilian_deaths_major }
				random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = yes
				CONTROLLER = {
					set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.abusive_operation_legitimacy_loss }
					multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = -1 }
					random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
					set_country_flag = random_terror_abusive_response_recorded
					random_terror_add_public_government_abuse_condemnation = yes
					random_terror_achievement_record_abusive_failure = yes
				}
			}
			# Survivor displacement (Part 4 Evolution I): a partial or costly
			# raid scatters the cell's survivors unless a defection channel or
			# allied intelligence keeps track of them.
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success }
						check_variable = { random_terror_raid_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.costly_success }
					}
					NOT = { has_state_flag = random_terror_joint_intelligence_received }
					ROOT = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_defection_channel_open } }
				}
				random_terror_mark_survivors_displaced = yes
			}
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_raid_target
		}
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_raid_outcome_recorded
	random_terror_check_for_defeat = yes
}

random_terror_resolve_support_disruption = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_recorded
			set_state_flag = random_terror_support_disruption_committed_@ROOT
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_activity_reduction }
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.global_network_intelligence_reduction }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_is_actor = yes
			has_country_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_active
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_action_network_gain }
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_active
		random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = random_terror_corridors_broken_count } }
		set_variable = { random_terror_corridors_broken_count = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_corridors_broken_count = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_support_disruption_resolved
	random_terror_check_for_defeat = yes
}

random_terror_open_allied_intelligence_request = {
	random_terror_initialize_country_state = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_received
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_refused
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			set_state_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_subject
		}
	}
	save_event_target_as = random_terror_requesting_country
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_country = {
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				command_power > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intelligence_command_power
				has_equipment = { support_equipment > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intelligence_support_equipment }
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = event_target:random_terror_requesting_country
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				command_power > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intelligence_command_power
				has_equipment = { support_equipment > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intelligence_support_equipment }
			}
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.40 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_refused
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_request_pending
		set_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_response_recorded
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_intelligence_request_recorded
}

random_terror_accept_allied_intelligence = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_received
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_refused
	set_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_response_recorded
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			set_state_flag = random_terror_joint_intelligence_received
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_activity_reduction }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
}

random_terror_refuse_allied_intelligence = {

	set_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_response_recorded

		}

}

# The committed reserve holds the state: while it stays, the state cannot slip
# into Lost Local Control (random_terror_update_state_stage), however high its
# activity climbs.
random_terror_commit_reinforcement = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_reinforcement_duration = constant:random_terror_tuning.reinforcement_days }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = { set_state_flag = { flag = random_terror_state_reinforced days = [REINFORCEMENT_DAYS] } }
				REINFORCEMENT_DAYS = "[?random_terror_reinforcement_duration|.0]"
			}
		}
	}
}

					random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
				}
			}
		}

			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }

}

# COUNTRY scope: the government.  Marks the negotiated state, then lets the
# holding command answer: an AI-run command accepts at once, a human-run one
# receives chaosx.nr31.46 (ROOT = command, FROM = this government) and is
# marked as weighing terms for at most random_terror_surrender.answer_days.
# The decision gate (random_terror_actor_open_to_surrender_terms) has
# already checked the command's weakness and route.
random_terror_begin_surrender_negotiation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			set_state_flag = random_terror_surrender_negotiation_active
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						random_terror_actor_accept_surrender_terms = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { random_terror_surrender_answer_duration = constant:random_terror_surrender.answer_days }
						meta_effect = {
							text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_surrender_terms_pending days = [ANSWER_DAYS] } }
							ANSWER_DAYS = "[?random_terror_surrender_answer_duration|.0]"
						}
						country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.46 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: a command accepting terms; needs event target
# random_terror_surrender_government.  The negotiated state is the owned state
# carrying random_terror_surrender_negotiation_active, saved before the
# command disarms (actor cleanup clears that flag and returns the state).
# The command disarms through random_terror_actor_surrender, the hardliners
# who reject the settlement stay there as a cell at
# random_terror_surrender.hardliner_activity, and the government earns the
# legitimacy of a settlement.
random_terror_actor_accept_surrender_terms = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_surrender_terms_pending
	random_owned_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_surrender_negotiation_active }
		save_event_target_as = random_terror_surrender_state
	}
	random_terror_actor_surrender = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_surrender_state }
		event_target:random_terror_surrender_state = {
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_surrender_negotiation_active
			set_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_surrender.hardliner_activity }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_surrender_government
			event_target:random_terror_surrender_government = { exists = yes }
		}
		event_target:random_terror_surrender_government = { random_terror_response_raise_legitimacy = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: a human-run command refusing terms (chaosx.nr31.46).  The
# negotiated state loses its talks marker, the offering government keeps its
# Response Legitimacy, and no new terms reach this command for
# random_terror_surrender.refusal_days.
random_terror_actor_refuse_surrender_terms = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_surrender_terms_pending
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_surrender_negotiation_active }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_surrender_negotiation_active
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_surrender_refusal_duration = constant:random_terror_surrender.refusal_days }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_surrender_terms_refused days = [REFUSAL_DAYS] } }
		REFUSAL_DAYS = "[?random_terror_surrender_refusal_duration|.0]"
	}
}

random_terror_open_intervention_request = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_accepted
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_refused
	save_event_target_as = random_terror_requesting_country
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_country = {
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				command_power > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intervention_command_power
				has_equipment = { convoy_1 > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intervention_convoys }
				has_equipment = { support_equipment > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intervention_support_equipment }
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = event_target:random_terror_requesting_country
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				command_power > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intervention_command_power
				has_equipment = { convoy_1 > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intervention_convoys }
				has_equipment = { support_equipment > constant:random_terror_cost.partner_intervention_support_equipment }
			}
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.41 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_refused
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_request_pending
		set_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_response_recorded
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = { set_state_flag = random_terror_intervention_subject }
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_request_recorded
}

random_terror_resolve_network_leadership_contest = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_contesting_authority = random_terror_network_authority }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_network_leader_target }
		event_target:random_terror_network_leader_target = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					random_terror_is_jihadist_actor = yes
					has_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader
					NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						ROOT = {
							check_variable = { random_terror_contesting_authority > random_terror_network_authority }
						}
						is_ai = yes
					}
					clr_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader
					ROOT = {
						set_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader
						set_country_flag = random_terror_network_leadership_won
					}
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = {
						set_country_flag = random_terror_network_leadership_contest_lost
						subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.network_leadership_contest_loss }
						random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
			has_country_flag = random_terror_network_leadership_contest_lost
			is_in_faction = yes
		}
		set_variable = { random_terror_achievement_split_members = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
				random_terror_is_jihadist_actor = yes
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_network_leader }
				ROOT = {
					check_variable = { random_terror_achievement_split_members < constant:random_terror_tuning.achievement_actor_split_count }
				}
			}
			leave_faction = yes
			set_country_flag = random_terror_split_from_international
			ROOT = { add_to_variable = { random_terror_achievement_split_members = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
		}
		leave_faction = yes
		set_country_flag = random_terror_rejected_international_command
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.global_unity_disruption }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
	random_terror_achievement_record_leadership_contest = yes
}

random_terror_commit_state_offensive = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			add_claim_by = ROOT
			set_state_flag = random_terror_transnational_link_active
			add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_offensive_activity_gain }
			add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.minor_pressure_gain }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
}

random_terror_commit_force_extraction = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_action_state }
		event_target:random_terror_action_state = {
			random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
			set_state_flag = random_terror_force_extraction_resolved
			add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.failed_operation_pressure_gain }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_tuning.civilian_deaths_minor }
			random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = yes
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_action_supply_gain }
	random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
}

random_terror_validate_jihad_transaction = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_valid
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_evolution_4_unlocked
			NOT = { random_terror_is_final_actor = yes }
			has_event_target = random_terror_jihadist_international_target
			event_target:random_terror_jihadist_international_target = {
				random_terror_is_jihadist_actor = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor }
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_valid
	}
}

random_terror_commit_jihad_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_valid }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_membership_request_pending
		save_event_target_as = random_terror_jihad_applicant
		event_target:random_terror_jihadist_international_target = {
			set_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_membership_offer_pending
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.44 }
		}
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_valid
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_valid }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_membership_request_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_failed
	}
}

random_terror_accept_jihad_membership = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_jihadist_international_member
	set_country_flag = random_terror_later_religious_opposition_registry
	set_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_committed
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_membership_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_failed
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.readiness_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_achievement_record_jihad_membership = yes
}

# Applicant scope, ROOT = the rejecting leader (chaosx.nr31.44).
random_terror_refuse_jihad_membership = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_refused_by_@ROOT
	set_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_failed
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_membership_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_transaction_committed
}

random_terror_commit_coordinated_uprising = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_uprising_state
			event_target:random_terror_uprising_state = {
				CONTROLLER = {
					check_variable = { random_terror_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.minimum_pressure_for_uprising }
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.uprising_readiness_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: an actor launching a coordinated uprising in event target
# random_terror_uprising_state, also saved as random_terror_action_state.
# The government holding the state receives Stop the Coordinated Uprising,
# the uprising starts where the state's pressure allows, the actor takes the
# state when its Territorial Control is high enough, and the network's
# readiness grows.  Call a Coordinated Uprising and the Open the Worldwide
# Uprising focus share it; callers pay their price first.
random_terror_launch_coordinated_uprising = {
	random_terror_actor_action_common = yes
	# Bind the defender before the uprising can change state control.
	event_target:random_terror_uprising_state = {
		save_event_target_as = random_terror_mission_subject
		CONTROLLER = { random_terror_begin_uprising_mission = yes }
	}
	event_target:random_terror_uprising_state = { random_terror_start_pressure_uprising = yes }
	random_terror_try_takeover_state = yes
	random_terror_commit_coordinated_uprising = yes
	random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
}

random_terror_validate_revelation_transaction = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_transaction_valid
	if = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_is_revelation_candidate = yes
			random_terror_can_begin_false_revelation = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_transaction_valid
	}
}

random_terror_commit_revelation_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_transaction_valid }
		random_terror_schedule_false_revelation = yes
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_transaction_valid
		set_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_transaction_committed
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_transaction_committed } }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_revelation_transaction_failed
	}
}

	set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_payload = global.random_terror_evolution_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_actor = THIS.id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_has_actor = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_secondary_actor = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_has_secondary_actor = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	record_events_log_system_history_entry = yes
	refresh_events_log_system_history_views = yes
}

		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_event_type = constant:event_system_event_type.repeatable }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_payload = global.random_terror_last_wave_affected_count }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_actor = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_has_actor = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_secondary_actor = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_system_has_secondary_actor = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		record_events_log_system_history_entry = yes
		refresh_events_log_system_history_views = yes
		set_global_flag = random_terror_automatic_wave_history_recorded
	}
}

random_terror_begin_containment_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_1
	set_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_containment
	set_variable = { random_terror_mission_target_containment = event_target:random_terror_mission_subject }
	set_variable = { random_terror_containment_start_hotspots = random_terror_hotspot_count }
	event_target:random_terror_mission_subject = {
		random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
		set_state_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_subject_@PREV
		set_variable = { random_terror_containment_stage_@PREV = random_terror_state_stage }
		set_variable = { random_terror_containment_goal_@PREV = random_terror_state_activity }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_containment_goal_@PREV = constant:random_terror_tuning.mission_containment_activity_target }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_containment_goal_@PREV min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	}
	activate_mission = random_terror_contain_attack_wave
}

random_terror_finish_containment_mission = {
	# Receivers without this mission skip the state scan.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_mission_target_containment }
		every_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_subject_@PREV }
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_subject_@PREV
			clear_variable = random_terror_containment_goal_@PREV
			clear_variable = random_terror_containment_stage_@PREV
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_1
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_security_surge_active
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_containment
	clear_variable = random_terror_mission_target_containment
	clear_variable = random_terror_containment_start_hotspots
}

# Civil authority is restored when residual activity falls by the restore
# target and never above the Active threshold, so a state already near Clear
# must be cleared fully.
random_terror_begin_restore_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_3
	set_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_restore
	set_variable = { random_terror_mission_target_restore = event_target:random_terror_mission_subject }
	event_target:random_terror_mission_subject = {
		random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
		set_state_flag = random_terror_restore_civil_authority_subject_@PREV
		set_variable = { random_terror_restore_start_@PREV = random_terror_state_activity }
		set_variable = { random_terror_restore_goal_@PREV = random_terror_state_activity }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_restore_goal_@PREV = constant:random_terror_tuning.mission_restore_activity_target }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_restore_goal_@PREV min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_active_threshold }
	}
	activate_mission = random_terror_restore_civil_authority
}

random_terror_finish_restore_mission = {
	# Receivers without this mission skip the state scan.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_mission_target_restore }
		every_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_restore_civil_authority_subject_@PREV }
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_restore_civil_authority_subject_@PREV
			clear_variable = random_terror_restore_start_@PREV
			clear_variable = random_terror_restore_goal_@PREV
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_3
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_restore
	clear_variable = random_terror_mission_target_restore
}

# Runs in the hostage state's controller, which receives the mission.

}

			clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject_@PREV
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_recovered_@PREV
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_partly_freed_@PREV
