# Runs in the uprising state's controller, which receives the mission; the
# calling actor is never the receiver.
random_terror_begin_uprising_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_2
	set_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_uprising
	set_variable = { random_terror_mission_target_uprising = event_target:random_terror_mission_subject }
	event_target:random_terror_mission_subject = {
		random_terror_initialize_state_state = yes
		set_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@PREV
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_stopped_@PREV
		set_variable = { random_terror_uprising_goal_@PREV = random_terror_state_activity }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_uprising_goal_@PREV = constant:random_terror_tuning.mission_uprising_activity_target }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_uprising_goal_@PREV min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	}
	activate_mission = random_terror_stop_coordinated_uprising
}

random_terror_finish_uprising_mission = {
	# Receivers without this mission skip the state scan.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_mission_target_uprising }
		every_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@PREV }
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@PREV
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_stopped_@PREV
			clear_variable = random_terror_uprising_goal_@PREV
			# The rising is over, whatever its result; only the date window
			# (random_terror_uprising_cooldown_until) still holds the state.
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_recent
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_2
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_active_uprising
	clear_variable = random_terror_mission_target_uprising
}

# COUNTRY scope: a mission receiver.  Moves national Terror Pressure by the
# outcome of a resolved mission.  Input: temp random_terror_mission_outcome
# (constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.*).
random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.mission_failure_pressure_gain }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.mission_success_pressure_reduction }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = -1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.mission_partial_pressure_reduction }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_pressure_delta = -1 }
	}
	random_terror_apply_pressure = yes
}

random_terror_cancel_containment_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_cancel_transport_protection_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_cancel_corridor_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_cancel_capital_security_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_cancel_retake_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_cancel_restore_civil_authority_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_cancel_hostage_deadline_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_cancel_uprising_mission = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_cancelled
}

random_terror_resolve_containment_mission_success = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_activity_reduction }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_gain }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	# A clean containment leaves a little lasting counterterror capacity.
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_lasting_capacity = constant:random_terror_capacity.containment_lasting_gain }
	random_terror_add_lasting_capacity = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_success
}

random_terror_resolve_containment_mission_partial = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_containment_partial
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_partial
}

random_terror_resolve_containment_mission_failure = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.failed_operation_pressure_gain }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_containment_failure
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_loss }
	multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = -1 }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_failure
}

random_terror_resolve_transport_protection_mission_success = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		random_terror_achievement_record_transport_restore = yes
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_transport_disrupted
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_port_disrupted
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.clean_operation_activity_reduction }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_gain }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_success
}

random_terror_resolve_corridor_mission_success = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_smuggling_corridor_recorded
		random_terror_achievement_record_corridor_broken = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_training_area_recorded }
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_training_area_recorded
			ROOT = { random_terror_achievement_record_safe_haven_eliminated = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active }
			OWNER = { random_terror_achievement_record_strategic_corridor = yes }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_tuning.global_network_intelligence_reduction }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_success
}

random_terror_resolve_corridor_mission_partial = {

		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_subject_@ROOT }

		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}

	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_partial

random_terror_resolve_capital_security_mission_success = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_capital_infiltration_recorded
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.capital_defense_activity_reduction }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_gain }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_success
	random_terror_achievement_record_capital_mission_success = yes
}

random_terror_trigger_coup_attempt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_coup_attempted }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_civil_war_started }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_civil_war_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = event021_civil_war_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active }
		}
		set_country_flag = random_terror_coup_attempted
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_coup_duration = constant:random_terror_tuning.coup_crisis_days }
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_coup_active days = [COUP_DAYS] }
				set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_government_paralysis days = [COUP_DAYS] }
			}
			COUP_DAYS = "[?random_terror_coup_duration|.0]"
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_coup_stability_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_stability_restriction_delta }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_coup_stability_delta = -1 }
		add_stability = random_terror_coup_stability_delta
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_loss }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = -1 }
		random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_size = constant:random_terror_tuning.coup_revolt_size }
		random_terror_start_pressure_civil_war = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_civil_war_begun = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_coup_successful
			set_country_flag = random_terror_government_paralysis_recorded
			random_terror_achievement_record_coup = yes
		}
	}
}

random_terror_resolve_capital_security_mission_partial = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_capital_security_partial
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_partial
}

random_terror_resolve_capital_security_mission_failure = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.failed_operation_pressure_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_capital_security_failure
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_loss }
	multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = -1 }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_success }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_coup_successful }
		}
		random_terror_trigger_coup_attempt = yes
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_failure
}

random_terror_resolve_retake_mission_success = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_subject_@ROOT
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
		}
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_retake_success
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery
		random_terror_achievement_record_original_capital_retake = yes
		OWNER = { random_terror_achievement_record_safe_haven_eliminated = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_terminal_command_capital }
			OWNER = { random_terror_achievement_record_command_capital = yes }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_retake_activity_reduction }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_strategic_victories = constant:random_terror_value.one }
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.readiness_gain }
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.global_unity_disruption }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_strategic_victories min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
	random_terror_schedule_false_revelation = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_success
}

		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_subject_@ROOT }

	}

}

random_terror_resolve_retake_mission_failure = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.failed_operation_pressure_gain }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_state_retake_failure
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		CONTROLLER = {
			if = {
				limit = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
				add_to_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_militia_gain }
				random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_failure
}

random_terror_resolve_restore_civil_authority_mission_success = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_restore_civil_authority_subject_@ROOT }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.high } }
			OWNER = { random_terror_achievement_record_restored_high_pressure = yes }
		}
		set_state_flag = random_terror_civil_authority_restored
		random_terror_achievement_record_civil_authority_restore = yes
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_activity_reduction }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		# A restored state rests longer before the network can strike it again.
		random_terror_mark_state_cooldown = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_gain }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_success
}

# Some captives are freed but the leaders escape: the crisis ends without a
# propaganda victory, and part of the local network survives.
random_terror_resolve_hostage_deadline_mission_partial = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_crisis_recorded
		set_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_resolution_partial
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_tuning.civilian_deaths_minor }
		random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = yes
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_hostage_deadline_partial
}

random_terror_resolve_hostage_deadline_mission_failure = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject_@ROOT }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_crisis_recorded
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_pressure_gain }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_tuning.civilian_deaths_major }
		random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = yes
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_resolution_failure
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_loss }
	multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = -1 }
	random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_hostage_deadline_failure
}

random_terror_resolve_uprising_mission_success = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@ROOT }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_recent
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_pressure_reduction }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.recovery_activity_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.uprising_readiness_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.clean_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_success
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active }
		random_terror_achievement_record_restored_high_pressure = yes
	}
}

random_terror_resolve_uprising_mission_partial = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@ROOT }
		subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.protected_pressure_reduction }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_partial
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.partial_success }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_partial
}

random_terror_resolve_uprising_mission_failure = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_subject_@ROOT }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_tuning.major_pressure_gain }
		add_to_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_tuning.state_activity_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_activity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_state_pressure min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		set_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_failure
		random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.uprising_readiness_gain }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_mission_outcome = constant:random_terror_operation_outcome.failure }
	random_terror_apply_mission_outcome_pressure = yes
	set_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_failure
}

random_terror_select_dominant_actor = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_dominant_score = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	clear_global_event_target = random_terror_dominant_actor
	every_country = {
		limit = { random_terror_is_jihadist_actor = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = random_terror_network_authority }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = random_terror_territorial_control }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score = random_terror_external_supply }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_candidate_score > random_terror_dominant_score } }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_dominant_score = random_terror_candidate_score }
			save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_dominant_actor
		}
	}
}

random_terror_mark_final_actor = {
	# Private commit helper: both callers are inside the full revelation gate.
	set_variable = { random_terror_pre_revelation_research_slots = amount_research_slots }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor
	set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_command
	set_country_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_active
	set_country_flag = random_terror_route_false_revelation
	set_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.final }
	set_research_slots = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_research_slots_final
	set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = no }
	JMX_random_terror_unanswered_presence = {
		set_nationality = PREV
		add_country_leader_role = {
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = { ideology = despotism traits = { } }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_control_cap }
	remove_ideas = {
		random_terror_improvised_command
		random_terror_captured_economy
		random_terror_contested_legitimacy
		random_terror_distributed_command
		random_terror_network_directorate
		random_terror_unified_field_command
		random_terror_directorate_general_staff
		random_terror_cadre_authority
		random_terror_ideological_state
		random_terror_external_supply_system
		random_terror_directed_war_economy
		random_terror_civil_administration
		random_terror_patron_development_mission
		random_terror_compromised_network
		random_terror_rival_warlords
		random_terror_doctrinal_schism
		random_terror_looting_spiral
		random_terror_famine
		random_terror_sponsor_dependency
		random_terror_security_state
		random_terror_claimed_authority
		random_terror_recognized_enclave
		random_terror_international_mandate
		random_terror_local_revolt
		random_terror_mass_defection
		random_terror_failed_rule
	}
	# The final strengths follow the world state through the dynamic
	# modifiers of random_terror_apply_terminal_strength, which the revelation
	# applies once the terminal phase is open; a flat Presence of the Entity
	# carried from Seal the Final Command gives way to them.
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = random_terror_presence_of_the_entity }
		remove_ideas = random_terror_presence_of_the_entity
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = random_terror_world_in_revolt }
		remove_ideas = random_terror_world_in_revolt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = random_terror_supply_through_ruin }
		remove_ideas = random_terror_supply_through_ruin
	}
	set_country_flag = random_terror_later_religious_opposition_registry
	random_terror_add_public_forced_rule_condemnation = yes
	random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
}

random_terror_schedule_false_revelation = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_can_begin_false_revelation = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_pending }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days = mtth:random_terror_false_revelation_days }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days min = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.minimum max = constant:random_terror_mtth_days.maximum }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_days = random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days }
		set_temp_variable = { chaosx_date_offset = constant:chaosx_date_units.zero }
		chaosx_convert_days_to_date_offset = yes
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_due_date = global.date }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_due_date = chaosx_date_offset }
		set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_pending
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_lapsed
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.94 days = random_terror_false_revelation_delay_days }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_pending
			NOT = { random_terror_can_begin_false_revelation = yes }
		}
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_pending
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_accelerated
		set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_lapsed
		clear_variable = global.random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_due_date
	}
}

random_terror_process_false_revelation_candidate = {
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_pending
	clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_accelerated
	clear_variable = global.random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_due_date
	random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_can_begin_false_revelation = yes }
		random_terror_begin_false_revelation = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_lapsed
	}
}

random_terror_begin_false_revelation = {
	random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_can_begin_false_revelation = yes }
		clear_global_event_target = random_terror_final_actor
		clear_global_event_target = random_terror_final_capital
		random_terror_release_dead_carriers = yes
		random_terror_select_dominant_actor = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = random_terror_dominant_actor
				event_target:random_terror_dominant_actor = {
					is_ai = yes
					random_terror_is_viable_actor = yes
					any_owned_state = { always = yes }
				}
				JMX = { random_terror_carrier_is_free = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_slot_jmx_used
			event_target:random_terror_dominant_actor = {
				random_owned_state = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_final_capital }
			}
			JMX = {
				# The final command keeps the technology of the command it absorbs.
				event_target:random_terror_dominant_actor = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_tech_donor }
				random_terror_inherit_actor_technology = yes
				annex_country = { target = event_target:random_terror_dominant_actor transfer_troops = yes }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = random_terror_actor
							has_country_flag = random_terror_actor_setup_complete
						}
					}
					random_terror_clear_actor_runtime = yes
				}
				set_capital = { state = event_target:random_terror_final_capital remember_old_capital = no }
				set_variable = { random_terror_actor_profile = constant:random_terror_actor_profile.final }
				random_terror_initialize_actor = yes
				random_terror_mark_final_actor = yes
				save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_final_actor
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = random_terror_dominant_actor
				event_target:random_terror_dominant_actor = {
					random_terror_is_viable_actor = yes
					any_owned_state = { always = yes }
				}
			}
			event_target:random_terror_dominant_actor = {
				capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_final_capital }
				random_terror_mark_final_actor = yes
				save_global_event_target_as = random_terror_final_actor
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_final_actor }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_contributions = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_final_capital }
				event_target:random_terror_final_capital = { set_state_flag = random_terror_terminal_command_capital }
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
			random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
			random_terror_refresh_terminal_strength = yes
			event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { random_terror_schedule_terminal_check = yes }
			event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { random_terror_assign_actor_cosmetic_tag = yes }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_pending
			set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_enabled
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_start_date = global.date }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_world_end_delay = constant:random_terror_tuning.world_end_delay_days }
			event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.95 days = random_terror_world_end_delay } }
			every_country = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.7 } }
		}
	}
}

random_terror_finalize_false_revelation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
			has_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_pending
			has_event_target = random_terror_final_actor
		}
		set_global_flag = world_end
		set_global_flag = world_end_random_terror_false_revelation
		set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_resolved
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_pending
		event_target:random_terror_final_actor = {
			set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_world_order
			random_terror_create_final_jihad_command_faction = yes
		}
		event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { random_terror_emit_super_event = yes }
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				random_terror_is_actor = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_subordinate
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_subordinate }
				set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_choice_honored
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_resister }
				set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_choice_honored
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_player_command
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_subordinate
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:random_terror_final_actor }
					event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				}
				random_terror_join_final_command = yes
			}
		}
		# Phase 1: the synchronized uprising wave, only in governments with
		# real groundwork (Part 8 Uprising system).
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_affected_countries^num > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.random_terror_affected_countries
				random_terror_revelation_uprising = yes
			}
		}
		random_terror_refresh_terminal_strength = yes
		set_global_flag = random_terror_world_end_ready
		random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: an actor submitting to the final command.  It leaves any
# other faction first; the final actor, as faction leader, then adds it
# (add_to_faction adds its target to the scope country's faction).
random_terror_join_final_command = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_final_actor
			event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { is_faction_leader = yes }
			NOT = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:random_terror_final_actor }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				dismantle_faction = yes
			}
			else = {
				leave_faction = yes
			}
		}
		event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { add_to_faction = PREV }
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Objective-based world-end power (Part 8 Final state ideas, Extreme abilities,
# Uprising system, Phase 1 and Global counterplay)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The final command's three strengths are dynamic modifiers whose values follow
# the world state.  random_terror_refresh_terminal_strength recomputes one
# 0-100 score for each, held between its floor and score_cap in the
# random_terror_terminal_strength constants:
#   Presence of the Entity  command capital held, surviving commands holding
#                           their own capitals, International Unity
#   World in Revolt         governments above the high-pressure gate (read
#                           from global.random_terror_affected_countries,
#                           never a world scan) and surviving commands
#   Supply Through Ruin     controlled ports and developed states, External
#                           Supply
# The hidden terminal check chaosx.nr31.98 refreshes the scores every
# check_days, the command-capital on_action refreshes them at once when the
# capital changes hands, and the Terminal Counteroffensive refreshes them
# after it strikes.  Scores are kept as globals for the tooltips.

# COUNTRY scope: marks a government as affected by Event 31 and lists it in
# global.random_terror_affected_countries, the bounded set read by the
# terminal strength check and the revelation uprising wave.
random_terror_mark_affected_country = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_affected_country
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = global.random_terror_affected_countries
					value = THIS
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = global.random_terror_affected_countries value = THIS }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: one Event 31 carrier tag.  A live command other than the
# final command counts as a surviving network, and as a command capital while
# it still controls its own capital.  Outputs (caller-owned temps):
# random_terror_terminal_networks and random_terror_terminal_capitals.
random_terror_count_terminal_command = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			random_terror_is_actor = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor }
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			num_of_controlled_states > constant:random_terror_value.zero
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_networks = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		if = {
			limit = { capital_scope = { is_controlled_by = PREV } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_capitals = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
	}
}

# Any scope.  Recomputes the three final strengths and refreshes the final
# command's modifiers.  Does nothing outside the terminal phase.
random_terror_refresh_terminal_strength = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
			has_event_target = random_terror_final_actor
			event_target:random_terror_final_actor = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
		}
		random_terror_initialize_global_state = yes
		# Surviving commands and their capitals, read from the fixed carrier tags.
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_networks = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_capitals = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = { limit = { JHX = { exists = yes } } JHX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		if = { limit = { JIX = { exists = yes } } JIX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		if = { limit = { JKX = { exists = yes } } JKX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		if = { limit = { JLX = { exists = yes } } JLX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		if = { limit = { JMX = { exists = yes } } JMX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		if = { limit = { JNX = { exists = yes } } JNX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		if = { limit = { JOX = { exists = yes } } JOX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		if = { limit = { JPX = { exists = yes } } JPX = { random_terror_count_terminal_command = yes } }
		# Governments above the high-pressure gate, from the affected list only.
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_pressured = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.random_terror_affected_countries^num > constant:random_terror_value.zero } }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.random_terror_affected_countries
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						has_country_flag = random_terror_affected_country
						NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_actor }
						check_variable = { random_terror_pressure > constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.high_pressure_gate }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_pressured = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_pressured_count = random_terror_terminal_pressured }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_network_count = random_terror_terminal_networks }
		set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_capital_held = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_event_target = random_terror_final_capital
				event_target:random_terror_final_capital = {
					has_state_flag = random_terror_terminal_command_capital
					CONTROLLER = { has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_capital_held = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		event_target:random_terror_final_actor = {
			# Presence of the Entity.
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = global.random_terror_terminal_capital_held }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.capital_points }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = random_terror_terminal_capitals }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.command_capital_points }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_part min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.command_capital_cap }
			add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = random_terror_terminal_part }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = global.random_terror_international_unity }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.unity_factor }
			add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = random_terror_terminal_part }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_final_command_sealed }
				add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.sealed_command_points }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_terminal_overlay }
				add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.focus_overlay_points }
			}
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_score min = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.presence_floor max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.score_cap }
			round_temp_variable = random_terror_terminal_score
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_presence_score = random_terror_terminal_score }
			# World in Revolt.
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = random_terror_terminal_pressured }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.pressured_country_points }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_score min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.pressured_country_cap }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = random_terror_terminal_networks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.network_points }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_part min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.network_cap }
			add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = random_terror_terminal_part }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_terminal_overlay }
				add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.focus_overlay_points }
			}
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_score min = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.revolt_floor max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.score_cap }
			round_temp_variable = random_terror_terminal_score
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_revolt_score = random_terror_terminal_score }
			# Supply Through Ruin.
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ports = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_developed = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			every_controlled_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { naval_base > constant:random_terror_value.zero }
					add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ports = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { infrastructure > constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.developed_infrastructure }
					add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_developed = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_port_count = random_terror_terminal_ports }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_developed_count = random_terror_terminal_developed }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = random_terror_terminal_ports }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.port_points }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_score min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.port_cap }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = random_terror_terminal_developed }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.developed_points }
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_part min = constant:random_terror_value.zero max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.developed_cap }
			add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = random_terror_terminal_part }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = random_terror_external_supply }
			multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_part = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.supply_factor }
			add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = random_terror_terminal_part }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_focus_terminal_overlay }
				add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_score = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.focus_overlay_points }
			}
			clamp_temp_variable = { var = random_terror_terminal_score min = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.ruin_floor max = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.score_cap }
			round_temp_variable = random_terror_terminal_score
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_ruin_score = random_terror_terminal_score }
			random_terror_apply_terminal_strength = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the final command.  Turns the three global scores into the
# variables read by its dynamic modifiers (score / score_cap x the full value
# in constants) and makes sure the modifiers are present and current.
random_terror_apply_terminal_strength = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ratio = global.random_terror_presence_score }
	divide_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ratio = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.score_cap }
	set_variable = { random_terror_presence_attack = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_presence_attack = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.presence_attack_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_presence_defence = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_presence_defence = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.presence_defence_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_presence_org = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_presence_org = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.presence_org_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_presence_reinforce = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_presence_reinforce = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.presence_reinforce_full }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ratio = global.random_terror_revolt_score }
	divide_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ratio = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.score_cap }
	set_variable = { random_terror_revolt_attack = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_revolt_attack = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.revolt_attack_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_revolt_war_support = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_revolt_war_support = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.revolt_war_support_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_revolt_political_power = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_revolt_political_power = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.revolt_political_power_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_revolt_conscription = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_revolt_conscription = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.revolt_conscription_full }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ratio = global.random_terror_ruin_score }
	divide_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_ratio = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.score_cap }
	set_variable = { random_terror_ruin_supply_consumption = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_ruin_supply_consumption = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.ruin_supply_consumption_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_ruin_out_of_supply = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_ruin_out_of_supply = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.ruin_out_of_supply_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_ruin_construction = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_ruin_construction = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.ruin_construction_full }
	set_variable = { random_terror_ruin_factory_output = random_terror_terminal_ratio }
	multiply_variable = { random_terror_ruin_factory_output = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.ruin_factory_output_full }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_presence } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_presence }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_revolt } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_revolt }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_ruin } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_ruin }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope: the final command.  Keeps one pending hidden terminal check
# (chaosx.nr31.98) for as long as the terminal phase lasts.
random_terror_schedule_terminal_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
			has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_check_pending }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_check_delay = constant:random_terror_terminal_strength.check_days }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_check_guard = random_terror_terminal_check_delay }
		add_to_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_check_guard = constant:random_terror_tuning.pulse_flag_buffer_days }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_terminal_check_pending days = [GUARD_DAYS] } }
			GUARD_DAYS = "[?random_terror_terminal_check_guard|.0]"
		}
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.98 days = random_terror_terminal_check_delay }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the hidden terminal check.
random_terror_run_terminal_check = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_check_pending
	random_terror_refresh_terminal_strength = yes
	random_terror_schedule_terminal_check = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope: a government in the opening uprising wave of the False
# Revelation (Part 8 Uprising system and Phase 1).  Only a government with
# Terror Pressure above the uprising gate and a pressured active state it
# controls rises; that state receives the ordinary pressure uprising and, when
# the government has a free emergency slot, the Stop the Coordinated Uprising
# mission.  A country with no pressure or no active cell receives nothing.
random_terror_revelation_uprising = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_actor }
			has_country_flag = random_terror_affected_country
			check_variable = { random_terror_pressure > constant:random_terror_value.minimum_pressure_for_uprising }
			any_controlled_state = { random_terror_state_can_rise_in_revelation = yes }
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { random_terror_state_can_rise_in_revelation = yes }
			save_event_target_as = random_terror_uprising_state
			if = {
				limit = {
					PREV = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_2 }
						random_terror_can_hold_response_mission = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = random_terror_mission_subject
				PREV = { random_terror_begin_uprising_mission = yes }
			}
			random_terror_start_pressure_uprising = yes
		}
	}
}

# STATE scope: an incident state where the network's fighters clashed with a
# neighbouring Event 14 cannibal warband (the incident draw requires one).
# Civilians caught between them die through the exact state-loss helper,
# which logs them in Deaths; the fighting damages local infrastructure; and
# the cannibal country draws public atrocity Condemnation.
random_terror_resolve_cannibal_clash = {
	set_temp_variable = { state_civilian_population_loss_applied = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_civilian_death_request = constant:random_terror_cannibal_rivalry.clash_deaths }
	random_terror_apply_exact_state_loss = yes
	set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_type = token:infrastructure }
	set_temp_variable = { dynamic_building_damage_amount = constant:random_terror_cannibal_rivalry.clash_building_damage }
	damage_state_building_dynamic = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_neighbor_state = {
				CONTROLLER = { is_cannibalism_country = yes }
			}
		}
		random_neighbor_state = {
			limit = {
				CONTROLLER = { is_cannibalism_country = yes }
			}
			CONTROLLER = { random_terror_add_cannibal_clash_condemnation = yes }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: an Event 14 cannibal country in a clash with Event 31 cells.
# Input: temp state_civilian_population_loss_applied from the clash.
random_terror_add_cannibal_clash_condemnation = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_source_type = constant:condemnation_source.atrocity }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_base_gain = constant:random_terror_cannibal_rivalry.clash_condemnation_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_visibility_type = constant:condemnation_visibility.public }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_severity_type = constant:condemnation_severity.major }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_civilian_deaths = state_civilian_population_loss_applied }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_context_type = constant:condemnation_context.unspecified }
	random_terror_add_condemnation_source = yes
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Safe havens and foreign-power choices (Part 4 Evolution II)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A host becomes a safe haven when its territory feeds the network: the
# source government of a cross-border spread, a country that agrees to
# sponsor an actor, and an actor that supports a foreign cell.  The marker is
# a visible dynamic modifier and adds Network Reach while it lasts.  Hosts
# are listed in global.random_terror_safe_haven_hosts, the target array of
# Press the Safe-Haven Host, so no check scans the world.  The marker clears
# when the host's crisis closes, when it accepts a demand, when its
# sponsorship is exposed, when an actor host is retired, and at defeat.

# COUNTRY scope: a host whose territory feeds spread or foreign-cell support.
random_terror_mark_safe_haven = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = random_terror_safe_haven } }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_safe_haven
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_safe_haven }
		add_to_array = { array = global.random_terror_safe_haven_hosts value = THIS }
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_safe_haven.reach_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: a host that no longer shelters the network.  The governments
# it fed lose their claim on it (random_terror_haven_link_<host>), so a host
# that becomes a safe haven again can be pressed only by the governments its
# new haven feeds.  The scan runs only when a marker actually clears.
random_terror_clear_safe_haven = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_safe_haven }
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_safe_haven
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_safe_haven }
		remove_from_array = { array = global.random_terror_safe_haven_hosts value = THIS }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_safe_haven.reach_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@PREV }
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@PREV
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the sponsor of an actor, inside random_terror_accept_sponsor_request
# where THIS is the actor and ROOT the sponsor.  The sponsor becomes a safe
# haven, and the government the actor rose against can press it.
random_terror_link_sponsor_haven = {
	ROOT = { random_terror_mark_safe_haven = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JHX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jhx
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jhx = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JIX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jix
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jix = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JKX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jkx
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jkx = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JLX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jlx
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jlx = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JMX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jmx
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jmx = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JNX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jnx
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jnx = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JOX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jox
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jox = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = JPX
			has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jpx
		}
		event_target:random_terror_parent_jpx = { set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_link_@ROOT }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the government pressing a host, ROOT of Press the Safe-Haven
# Host, with FROM = the host (the decision target) also saved as event target
# random_terror_haven_host.  Sends the host the demand (chaosx.nr31.45) and
# remembers the host for memory_days (random_terror_haven_pressed_<host>).
random_terror_commit_haven_demand = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_demand_pending
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_haven_memory_duration = constant:random_terror_safe_haven.memory_days }
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_haven_pressed_@FROM days = [MEMORY_DAYS] } }
		MEMORY_DAYS = "[?random_terror_haven_memory_duration|.0]"
	}
	event_target:random_terror_haven_host = {
		set_country_flag = random_terror_haven_demand_received
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.45 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the host accepting a demand (chaosx.nr31.45, ROOT = host,
# FROM = the demanding government).  It cracks down on the cells it shelters:
# its own active crisis states lose activity, Network Reach falls, and the
# marker clears.  The demanding government gains Response Legitimacy.
random_terror_accept_haven_demand = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_haven_demand_received
	add_stability = constant:random_terror_safe_haven.accept_host_stability
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = { random_terror_state_is_active_crisis = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_safe_haven.accept_activity_reduction }
		multiply_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_activity_delta = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
		random_terror_add_state_activity = yes
	}
	subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_network_reach = constant:random_terror_safe_haven.accept_reach_reduction }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_network_reach min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
	random_terror_clear_safe_haven = yes
	FROM = {
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_haven_demand_pending
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_pending_legitimacy_delta = constant:random_terror_tuning.response_legitimacy_gain }
		random_terror_apply_legitimacy = yes
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = PREV modifier = random_terror_safe_haven_cooperation }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope: the host refusing a demand.  It keeps the marker, draws
# public Condemnation for sheltering the network, and the demanding
# government's opinion of it falls.
random_terror_refuse_haven_demand = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_haven_demand_received
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_source_type = constant:condemnation_source.atrocity }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_base_gain = constant:random_terror_safe_haven.refuse_condemnation_gain }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_visibility_type = constant:condemnation_visibility.public }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_severity_type = constant:condemnation_severity.minor }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_civilian_deaths = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_condemnation_context_type = constant:condemnation_context.observer_report }
	random_terror_add_condemnation_source = yes
	FROM = {
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_haven_demand_pending
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = PREV modifier = random_terror_safe_haven_refusal }
	}
}

random_terror_emit_super_event = {
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_super_event_id = constant:random_terror_identity.reveal_super_event_id }
	set_temp_variable = { random_terror_super_event_duration_days = constant:random_terror_tuning.super_event_duration_days }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_aftermath_active }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_super_event_id = constant:random_terror_identity.defeat_super_event_id }
	}
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			set_global_flag = {
				flag = super_event_visible
				days = [RT_SUPER_EVENT_DURATION_DAYS]
				value = [RT_SUPER_EVENT_ID]
			}
		}
		RT_SUPER_EVENT_ID = "[?random_terror_super_event_id|.0]"
		RT_SUPER_EVENT_DURATION_DAYS = "[?random_terror_super_event_duration_days|.0]"
	}
	set_variable = { global.current_super_event_audio_id = random_terror_super_event_id }
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		play_current_super_event_audio = yes
	}
}

random_terror_clear_state_commitments = {
	# Cleanup leaves the staged modifier matching the state's remaining stage.
	random_terror_refresh_state_modifier = yes
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_joint_intelligence_received
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_civil_administration_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_community_protection_committed
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_containment_success
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_enclave_isolated
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_enclave_isolation_committed
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_external_corridor_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_force_extraction_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_foreign_cell_support_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_foreign_cell_supported
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_foreign_cell_support_resolved
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_force_extraction_resolved
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostages_recovered
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_hostage_crisis_resolved
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_intelligence_sweep_completed
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_intervention_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_intervention_accepted
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_militia_conversion_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_militia_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_network_relocation_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_raid_target
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_reinforcement_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_reinforcement_committed
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_relief_corridor_active
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_relief_corridor_subject
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_retake_commitment_active
	clr_state_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_subject

# COUNTRY scope: one receiver.  Removes its missions and clears only its own
# receipts through the family finish helpers, even when the caller's ROOT is
# another country.
random_terror_clear_country_commitments = {
	remove_mission = random_terror_contain_attack_wave
	remove_mission = random_terror_protect_transport_network
	remove_mission = random_terror_break_cross_border_corridor
	remove_mission = random_terror_prevent_capital_seizure
	remove_mission = random_terror_retake_lost_state
	remove_mission = random_terror_restore_civil_authority
	remove_mission = random_terror_hostage_deadline
	remove_mission = random_terror_stop_coordinated_uprising
	random_terror_finish_containment_mission = yes
	random_terror_finish_transport_mission = yes
	random_terror_finish_corridor_mission = yes
	random_terror_finish_capital_mission = yes
	random_terror_finish_retake_mission = yes
	random_terror_finish_restore_mission = yes
	random_terror_finish_hostage_mission = yes
	random_terror_finish_uprising_mission = yes
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_1
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_2
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_mission_slot_3
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_military_reinforcement_active
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_reinforcement_commitment_active
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_relief_corridor_active
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_relief_corridor_committed
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_requested
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_accepted
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_intervention_refused
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_received
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_allied_intelligence_refused
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_recorded
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_action_recent
}

# COUNTRY scope: an affected government.  Closes its crisis once the
# recovery obligations end (random_terror_country_recovery_complete): the
# country receives one short recovery report, sits out new attack waves for
# a while, and every per-crisis value, marker and response guard resets so a
# later crisis can run every response again.
random_terror_close_country_recovery = {
	if = {
		limit = { random_terror_country_recovery_complete = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			random_terror_achievement_finalize_country = yes
		}
		random_terror_clear_country_commitments = yes
		set_country_flag = random_terror_cycle_recovery_complete
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_recovery_cooldown_days = constant:random_terror_recovery.cooldown_days }
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_last_cycle_reformed
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_security_reform_complete }
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_recovery_cooldown_days = constant:random_terror_recovery.reformed_cooldown_days }
			set_country_flag = random_terror_last_cycle_reformed
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_country_flag = { flag = random_terror_recovery_cooldown days = [COOLDOWN_DAYS] } }
			COOLDOWN_DAYS = "[?random_terror_recovery_cooldown_days|.0]"
		}
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_cycle_incidents = random_terror_cycle_incident_count }
		set_variable = { random_terror_last_cycle_legitimacy = random_terror_response_legitimacy }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_recovery_report_pending
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.9 }
		random_terror_reset_crisis_cycle = yes
		random_terror_refresh_priority_state = yes
	}
	random_terror_reset_repeatable_cycle = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope: clears what one crisis cycle recorded for a government, so a
# later crisis in the same country starts fresh.  Lifetime achievement
# records are kept.
random_terror_reset_crisis_cycle = {
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_reconstruction_available
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_affected_country
	remove_from_array = { array = global.random_terror_affected_countries value = THIS }
	random_terror_clear_safe_haven = yes
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_haven_demand_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_report_queued
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_emphasis_chosen
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_response_cell_active
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_response_action_recorded
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_services_restored
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_public_accounting_complete
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_security_reform_complete
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_intelligence_breakthrough
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_defection_channel_open
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_quiet_leverage_available
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_cycle_civilian_loss
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_civil_war_started
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_coup_attempted
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_coup_successful
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_failed_raid_memory
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_sponsor_exposed
	# The pending recovery check is kept: a check already scheduled either
	# finds the country at rest and stops, or carries on a crisis that
	# reopened before it fired, so two chains never run at once.
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_success
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_partial
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_failure
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_containment_mission_cancelled
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_success
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_partial
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_failure
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_transport_mission_cancelled
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_success
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_partial
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_failure
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_corridor_mission_cancelled
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_success
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_partial
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_failure
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_capital_mission_cancelled
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_success
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_partial
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_failure
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_retake_mission_cancelled
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_success
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_partial
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_failure
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_restore_mission_cancelled
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_hostage_mission_cancelled
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_success
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_partial
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_failure
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_uprising_mission_cancelled
	clear_variable = random_terror_pressure
	clear_variable = random_terror_response_legitimacy
	# The crisis capacity ends with the crisis; the lasting part stays.
	set_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = random_terror_lasting_capacity }
		set_variable = { random_terror_response_capacity = random_terror_lasting_capacity }
	}
	clear_variable = random_terror_cycle_incident_count
	clear_variable = random_terror_spread_ready_date
	# A closed crisis leaves no rising under way in the country's own states.
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_recent }
		clr_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_recent
	}
}

random_terror_reset_repeatable_cycle = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_resolved
			NOT = { has_global_flag = world_end }
			NOT = { random_terror_has_unresolved_actor = yes }
			NOT = { any_country = { has_country_flag = random_terror_reconstruction_available } }
		}
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_resolved
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_aftermath_active
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_wave_news_sent
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_network_defeat_news_sent
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_scenario_news_sent
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_active
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_world_end_ready
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_pending
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_accelerated
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_lapsed
		clear_variable = global.random_terror_false_revelation_candidate_due_date
		clear_variable = global.random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_contributions
		clear_global_event_target = random_terror_final_actor
		clear_global_event_target = random_terror_final_capital
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_achievement_ledger_initialized }
			random_terror_achievement_reset_connected_route = yes
		}
		set_global_flag = random_terror_repeatable_cycle_rearmed
		random_terror_refresh_global_values = yes
	}
}

random_terror_resolve_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_resolved } }
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_defeat = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
					has_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_denied
					has_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_resolved
					has_global_flag = world_end_random_terror_false_revelation
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_terminal_defeat = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_terminal_defeat = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
			every_country = {
				random_terror_achievement_record_terminal_cleanup = yes
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_resolved
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_global_jihad_active
		clr_global_flag = random_terror_final_jihad_active
		clear_variable = global.random_terror_presence_score
		clear_variable = global.random_terror_revolt_score
		clear_variable = global.random_terror_ruin_score
		every_country = {
			random_terror_clear_country_commitments = yes
			random_terror_clear_safe_haven = yes
			if = {
				limit = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
				random_terror_cleanup_actor = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_affected_country }
				set_country_flag = random_terror_reconstruction_available
				random_terror_schedule_recovery_check = yes
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			random_terror_clear_state_commitments = yes
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { is_ai = no exists = yes }
			random_terror_achievement_finalize_country = yes
		}
		random_terror_scenario_cleanup = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_terminal_defeat = constant:random_terror_value.one } }
			set_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_aftermath_active
			every_country = { limit = { is_ai = no exists = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr31.8 } }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_broken_claim_news_sent } }
				set_global_flag = random_terror_broken_claim_news_sent
				news_event = { id = chaosx.news.314 days = 1 }
			}
			random_terror_emit_super_event = yes
		}
		else = {
			clr_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_aftermath_active
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_network_defeat_news_sent } }
				news_event = { id = chaosx.news.315 }
				set_global_flag = random_terror_network_defeat_news_sent
			}
		}
		random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
	}
}
