	clr_country_flag = random_terror_ideological_mobilization_recorded
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_negotiated_surrender
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_disarmament_terms_active
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_surrender_terms_pending
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_surrender_terms_refused
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_actor_atrocity_condemnation_recorded
	# Per-target transaction records between this command and every other
	# live command, in both directions, so a reused carrier starts clean.
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_actor }
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_absorption_refused_by_@PREV
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_refused_by_@PREV
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_leader_contested_@PREV
		PREV = {
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_absorption_refused_by_@PREV
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_jihad_refused_by_@PREV
			clr_country_flag = random_terror_leader_contested_@PREV
		}
	}
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_mobilized_cadres }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_synchronized_offensive }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_presence }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_revolt }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = random_terror_entity_ruin }
	random_terror_clear_safe_haven = yes
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_advanced_research_slot
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_check_pending
	clear_variable = random_terror_presence_attack
	clear_variable = random_terror_presence_defence
	clear_variable = random_terror_presence_org
	clear_variable = random_terror_presence_reinforce
	clear_variable = random_terror_revolt_attack
	clear_variable = random_terror_revolt_war_support
	clear_variable = random_terror_revolt_political_power
	clear_variable = random_terror_revolt_conscription
	clear_variable = random_terror_ruin_supply_consumption
	clear_variable = random_terror_ruin_out_of_supply
	clear_variable = random_terror_ruin_construction
	clear_variable = random_terror_ruin_factory_output
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_parent_of_@PREV }
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_parent_of_@PREV
	}
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_compromised_network
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_rival_warlords
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_looting_spiral
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_famine
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_doctrinal_schism
	clr_country_flag = random_terror_lifecycle_owns_sponsor_dependency
	clear_variable = random_terror_actor_profile
	clear_variable = random_terror_network_authority
	clear_variable = random_terror_external_supply
	clear_variable = random_terror_territorial_control
	drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JHX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_01 }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JIX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_02 }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JKX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_03 }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JLX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_04 }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JMX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_05 }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JNX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_06 }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JOX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_07 }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JPX } set_cosmetic_tag = event31_dormant_carrier_08 }
}

# COUNTRY scope: an AI actor being retired.  Its states go to its living
# parent.  Without one, each state goes to a living government outside the
# crisis that holds its core, otherwise to the actor's strongest enemy by
# division count.  A state nobody can take stays with the actor.
random_terror_return_actor_states = {
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JHX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jhx event_target:random_terror_parent_jhx = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jhx = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JIX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jix event_target:random_terror_parent_jix = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jix = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JKX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jkx event_target:random_terror_parent_jkx = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jkx = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JLX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jlx event_target:random_terror_parent_jlx = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jlx = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JMX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jmx event_target:random_terror_parent_jmx = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jmx = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JNX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jnx event_target:random_terror_parent_jnx = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jnx = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JOX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jox event_target:random_terror_parent_jox = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jox = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = { limit = { original_tag = JPX has_event_target = random_terror_parent_jpx event_target:random_terror_parent_jpx = { exists = yes } } event_target:random_terror_parent_jpx = { save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_parent } }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = random_terror_cleanup_parent }
		every_owned_state = { transfer_state_to = event_target:random_terror_cleanup_parent }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cleanup_best_divisions = constant:random_terror_value.negative_one }
		every_enemy_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cleanup_divisions = num_divisions }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { random_terror_cleanup_divisions > random_terror_cleanup_best_divisions } }
				set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cleanup_best_divisions = random_terror_cleanup_divisions }
				save_event_target_as = random_terror_cleanup_enemy
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cleanup_state_placed = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = core_countries
				break = random_terror_cleanup_state_placed
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						NOT = { random_terror_is_actor = yes }
					}
					transfer_state = PREV
					set_temp_variable = { random_terror_cleanup_state_placed = constant:random_terror_value.one }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { random_terror_cleanup_state_placed = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
					has_event_target = random_terror_cleanup_enemy
				}
				transfer_state_to = event_target:random_terror_cleanup_enemy
			}
		}
	}
}

random_terror_cleanup_actor = {
	random_terror_clear_country_commitments = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = random_terror_pre_revelation_research_slots }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { set_research_slots = [RESEARCH_SLOTS] }
				RESEARCH_SLOTS = "[?random_terror_pre_revelation_research_slots|.0]"
			}
			clear_variable = random_terror_pre_revelation_research_slots
		}
		JMX_random_terror_unanswered_presence = {
			remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = despotism }
			set_nationality = JMX
		}
	}
	save_event_target_as = random_terror_achievement_defeated_actor
	every_enemy_country = { random_terror_achievement_record_event31_actor_defeat_contribution = yes }
	every_owned_state = { random_terror_clear_state_commitments = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = yes }
		random_terror_return_actor_states = yes
	}
	# An AI actor that still owns states nobody could take keeps its Event 31
	# identity and units, so the crisis can still defeat or absorb it later.
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			NOT = { any_owned_state = { is_owned_by = PREV } }
		}
		delete_units = { disband = no }
		remove_ideas = {
			random_terror_improvised_command
			random_terror_captured_economy
			random_terror_contested_legitimacy
			random_terror_presence_of_the_entity
			random_terror_world_in_revolt
			random_terror_supply_through_ruin
		}
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_actor
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_active_actor
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_command
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_world_order
		set_country_flag = random_terror_cleanup_complete
		clear_variable = random_terror_network_authority
		clear_variable = random_terror_external_supply
		clear_variable = random_terror_territorial_control
		random_terror_clear_actor_runtime = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		# A human-controlled carrier is never deleted or stripped of units.  It is
		# retired in place after surrender/defeat so a multiplayer participant can
		# keep a valid country scope while the shared scenario closes.
		set_country_flag = random_terror_player_actor_defeat_resolved
		remove_ideas = {
			random_terror_improvised_command
			random_terror_captured_economy
			random_terror_contested_legitimacy
			random_terror_presence_of_the_entity
			random_terror_world_in_revolt
			random_terror_supply_through_ruin
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_lost_local_control
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_state_recovery
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_uprising_recent
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_territorial_takeover_recorded
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_territorial_partition_recorded
			clr_state_flag = random_terror_scenario_actor_state_reserved
			# A retired command's own states leave the crisis entirely.
			set_variable = { random_terror_state_activity = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			set_variable = { random_terror_state_pressure = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
			random_terror_update_state_stage = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_actor
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_active_actor
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_final_actor
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_command
		clr_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_world_order
		set_country_flag = random_terror_cleanup_complete
		clear_variable = random_terror_network_authority
		clear_variable = random_terror_external_supply
		clear_variable = random_terror_territorial_control
		random_terror_clear_actor_runtime = yes
	}
	random_terror_refresh_world_threat_source = yes
}

random_terror_actor_surrender = {
	set_country_flag = random_terror_negotiated_surrender
	subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_cost.surrender_legitimacy }
	subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_cost.surrender_command_power }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_network_authority min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_authority_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = random_terror_external_supply min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_tuning.actor_supply_cap }
	set_country_flag = random_terror_disarmament_terms_active
	save_event_target_as = random_terror_achievement_defeated_actor
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = random_terror_surrender_government
			event_target:random_terror_surrender_government = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:random_terror_achievement_defeated_actor }
			}
		}
		event_target:random_terror_surrender_government = {
			random_terror_achievement_record_event31_actor_defeat_contribution = yes
		}
	}
	random_terror_cleanup_actor = yes
	random_terror_check_for_defeat = yes
}

random_terror_check_for_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { random_terror_has_unresolved_actor = yes }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = random_terror_defeat_resolved }
		}
		random_terror_resolve_defeat = yes
	}
}

random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
			has_event_target = random_terror_final_actor
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness = constant:random_terror_tuning.readiness_gain }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.random_terror_international_unity = constant:random_terror_tuning.unity_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_apocalyptic_readiness min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
		clamp_variable = { var = global.random_terror_international_unity min = constant:random_terror_value.minimum max = constant:random_terror_value.maximum }
			event_target:random_terror_final_actor = {
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_territorial_control = constant:random_terror_tuning.terminal_denial_control_loss }
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_network_authority = constant:random_terror_tuning.terminal_denial_authority_loss }
			subtract_from_variable = { random_terror_external_supply = constant:random_terror_tuning.terminal_denial_supply_loss }
			random_terror_clamp_actor_values = yes
			set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_denial_pressure
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_contributions } }
			set_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_contributions = constant:random_terror_value.zero }
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_contributions = constant:random_terror_value.one }
		set_country_flag = random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_recorded
		random_terror_achievement_record_terminal_contribution = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.random_terror_terminal_counteroffensive_contributions value = constant:random_terror_cost.terminal_counteroffensive_required_contributions compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				event_target:random_terror_final_actor = {
					random_terror_is_final_actor = yes
					random_terror_has_terminal_losses = yes
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_denied
			clr_global_flag = random_terror_false_revelation_pending
			clr_global_flag = random_terror_terminal_active
			event_target:random_terror_final_actor = {
				set_country_flag = random_terror_cleanup_requested
				random_terror_cleanup_actor = yes
			}
			random_terror_check_for_defeat = yes
		}
		# The blow shows at once in the final strengths.
		random_terror_refresh_terminal_strength = yes
	}
}

# CXT extension contract.  The hidden carrier idea is registered by the
# additive startup/daily hooks in 031_random_terror_cxt_on_actions.txt.
random_terror_register_cxt_test_content = {
	set_temp_variable = { chaosx_test_country_registration_extension_effect = token:chaosx_cxt_extension_event031_random_terror }
	chaosx_test_country_register_extension_effect = yes
}

chaosx_cxt_extension_event031_random_terror_apply = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = chaosx_cxt_extension_event031_random_terror_applied } }
		set_global_flag = chaosx_cxt_extension_event031_random_terror_applied
	}
	set_country_flag = chaosx_cxt_extension_event031_random_terror_available
}
